- [ ] **Step 2: Implement the edit plumbing (top of `src/ui/app/edit.rs`)**

```rust
use super::*;
use crate::model::{Denial, EditContext, OpKind, check};
use crate::ops::{Confirmed, Op, OpId};
use std::time::Instant;

/// An edit GitHub refused, for the error log.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub struct FailedEdit {
    pub kind: OpKind,
    pub op: Op,
    /// What the edit was, e.g. "set Status to Done on 'Fix crash'".
    pub what: String,
    /// GitHub's own message.
    pub message: String,
    pub at: Instant,
}

/// A yes/no question on the status line (`Mode::Confirm`).
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub struct Confirm {
    pub question: String,
    pub action: ConfirmAction,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub enum ConfirmAction {
    Quit,
}

/// GitHub's own words for a failure; other errors as their message.
pub fn error_message(error: &GithubError) -> String {
    match error {
        GithubError::Graphql(entries) if !entries.is_empty() => entries
            .iter()
            .map(|e| e.message.as_str())
            .collect::<Vec<_>>()
            .join("; "),
        other => other.to_string(),
    }
}

impl App {
    pub fn edit_context(&self) -> EditContext {
        EditContext {
            viewer_can_update: self.project().is_some_and(|p| p.viewer_can_update),
            scopes: self.scopes.clone(),
            refused: self.refused_scopes.clone(),
        }
    }

    pub fn pending_count(&self) -> usize {
        self.store.pending().len()
    }

    /// The item edit keys act on: the open detail's item, else the selected one.
    pub fn action_item(&self) -> Option<&Item> {
        if self.detail.is_some() {
            self.detail_item()
        } else {
            self.selected_item()
        }
    }

    /// The mode a popover or question returns to.
    pub fn base_mode(&self) -> Mode {
        if self.detail.is_some() {
            Mode::Detail
        } else {
            Mode::Normal
        }
    }

    pub fn denial_text(&self, denial: Denial) -> String {
        denial.message(&|kind| {
            Action::for_kind(kind)
                .and_then(|a| self.keymap.keys_for(a).first().map(|k| k.label()))
        })
    }

    /// Runs `op` when the capability matrix allows `kind` on its item: the edit shows at once
    /// and `Command::Send` asks the controller to send it. Otherwise shows the reason.
    pub(crate) fn submit(&mut self, kind: OpKind, op: Op) -> Vec<Command> {
        let Some(item) = self.snapshot().and_then(|s| s.items.get(op.item())).cloned() else {
            self.status.flash = Some("That item is no longer on the board".into());
            return Vec::new();
        };
        if let Err(denial) = check(kind, &item, &self.edit_context()) {
            self.status.flash = Some(self.denial_text(denial));
            return Vec::new();
        }
        let id = self.store.begin(op);
        self.pending_kinds.insert(id, kind);
        self.restore_selection();
        vec![Command::Send(id)]
    }

    /// GitHub answered edit `id`. A failure leaves the pending list (the view rebuilds
    /// without it), shows GitHub's message and goes to the error log.
    pub(crate) fn settle(&mut self, id: OpId, result: Result<Confirmed, GithubError>) {
        let kind = self.pending_kinds.remove(&id);
        match result {
            Ok(confirmed) => {
                self.store.settle_ok(id, confirmed);
            }
            Err(error) => {
                let what = self
                    .store
                    .pending()
                    .iter()
                    .find(|p| p.id == id)
                    .map(|p| p.op.describe(self.snapshot()))
                    .unwrap_or_else(|| "edit".into());
                let Some(op) = self.store.settle_failed(id) else {
                    return;
                };
                if let GithubError::InsufficientScopes { missing } = &error {
                    self.refused_scopes.extend(missing.iter().cloned());
                }
                let message = error_message(&error);
                self.status.error = Some(format!("Could not {what}: {message}"));
                self.errors.push(FailedEdit {
                    kind: kind.unwrap_or(OpKind::SetField),
                    op,
                    what,
                    message,
                    at: Instant::now(),
                });
            }
        }
    }

    /// Quits, or asks first while edits are still sending.
    pub(crate) fn request_quit(&mut self) -> Vec<Command> {
        let n = self.pending_count();
        if n == 0 {
            self.quit = true;
            return vec![Command::Quit];
        }
        let (s, verb) = if n == 1 { ("", "is") } else { ("s", "are") };
        self.confirm = Some(Confirm {
            question: format!("{n} edit{s} {verb} still sending; quit anyway? (y/n)"),
            action: ConfirmAction::Quit,
        });
        self.mode = Mode::Confirm;
        Vec::new()
    }

    pub(crate) fn handle_confirm_key(&mut self, key: &KeyEvent) -> Vec<Command> {
        let Some(confirm) = self.confirm.take() else {
            self.mode = self.base_mode();
            return Vec::new();
        };
        match key.code {
            KeyCode::Char('y') | KeyCode::Char('Y') | KeyCode::Enter => {
                self.mode = self.base_mode();
                self.run_confirmed(confirm.action)
            }
            KeyCode::Char('n') | KeyCode::Char('N') | KeyCode::Esc => {
                self.mode = self.base_mode();
                Vec::new()
            }
            _ => {
                self.confirm = Some(confirm);
                Vec::new()
            }
        }
    }

    fn run_confirmed(&mut self, action: ConfirmAction) -> Vec<Command> {
        match action {
            ConfirmAction::Quit => {
                self.quit = true;
                vec![Command::Quit]
            }
        }
    }
}
```

```rust
/// An App over the UI fixture board, for tests in this module's children.
#[cfg(test)]
pub(crate) fn test_app() -> App {
    App::new(
        Box::new(crate::store::MemoryStore::new(Some(crate::ui::fixtures::snapshot()))),
```

```rust
use crate::model::OpKind;

impl Action {
    /// The operations this action's key runs; empty for actions that edit nothing. Later
    /// tasks fill this in as they add edit keys.
    pub fn kinds(self) -> &'static [OpKind] {
        &[]
    }

    /// The action whose key runs `kind`, for hints such as "(C)" in messages.
    pub fn for_kind(kind: OpKind) -> Option<Action> {
        Action::ALL.iter().copied().find(|a| a.kinds().contains(&kind))
    }
}
```

### Task 10: The controller sends edits through the queue

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/ui/controller.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `OpQueue` (Task 7); `Command::Send`, `App::submit`, `Status.write_pause` (Task 9); `SyncJob::Mutate`, `SyncEvent::OpDone` (Task 8).
- Produces: `Controller::commands` becomes `pub(crate)`; every `Command::Send` enters the queue and becomes `SyncJob::Mutate` effects as the queue allows; a rate-limited `OpDone` is retried after the pause; finished edits persist the confirmed cache; `Input::Tick` dispatches once a pause ends; opening another board clears the queue.

```rust
    use crate::model::OpKind;
    use crate::ops::{Confirmed, Op, OpId};

    fn opened(dir: &Path) -> (Controller, Instant) {
        save_cache(&cache_path(dir, &board()), &snapshot()).unwrap();
        let (mut c, _, t0) = controller(dir, Some("tviles/3"));
        c.start(Ok(()), t0);
        (c, t0)
    }

    fn set_status(item: &str, option: &str) -> Op {
        Op::SetField {
            item: ItemId::new(item),
            field: FieldId::new("F_status"),
            value: FieldValue::SingleSelect { option_id: OptionId::new(option), name: option.into() },
        }
    }

    fn submit(c: &mut Controller, op: Op, now: Instant) -> Vec<Effect> {
        let cmds = c.app.submit(OpKind::Move, op);
        c.commands(cmds, now)
    }

    fn mutates(effects: &[Effect]) -> Vec<OpId> {
        effects
            .iter()
            .filter_map(|e| match e {
                Effect::Spawn { job: SyncJob::Mutate { id, .. }, .. } => Some(*id),
                _ => None,
            })
            .collect()
    }

    fn done(c: &mut Controller, id: OpId, result: Result<Confirmed, GithubError>, now: Instant) -> Vec<Effect> {
        let generation = c.generation();
        c.handle(Input::Sync { generation, event: SyncEvent::OpDone { id, result } }, now)
    }

    #[test]
    fn edits_to_different_items_run_four_at_a_time() {
        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let (mut c, t0) = opened(dir.path());
        let mut sent = Vec::new();
        for item in ["a", "b", "c", "d", "e"] {
            sent.extend(mutates(&submit(&mut c, set_status(item, "o_done"), t0)));
        }
        assert_eq!(sent, [1, 2, 3, 4]);
        assert_eq!(mutates(&done(&mut c, 2, Ok(Confirmed::Applied), t0)), [5]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_second_edit_to_the_same_item_waits_for_the_first() {
        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let (mut c, t0) = opened(dir.path());
        assert_eq!(mutates(&submit(&mut c, set_status("a", "o_prog"), t0)), [1]);
        assert!(mutates(&submit(&mut c, set_status("a", "o_done"), t0)).is_empty());
        let error = GithubError::Network("offline".into());
        assert_eq!(mutates(&done(&mut c, 1, Err(error), t0)), [2], "sent even though 1 failed");
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_rate_limited_edit_is_retried_after_the_pause() {
        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let (mut c, t0) = opened(dir.path());
        submit(&mut c, set_status("a", "o_done"), t0);
        let limited = GithubError::RateLimited { retry_after_secs: 30 };
        assert!(done(&mut c, 1, Err(limited), t0).is_empty());
        assert_eq!(c.app.pending_count(), 1, "still pending, not failed");
        assert!(c.app.errors.is_empty());
        assert_eq!(c.app.status.write_pause, Some(t0 + Duration::from_secs(30)));
        assert!(mutates(&c.handle(Input::Tick, t0 + Duration::from_secs(29))).is_empty());
        assert_eq!(mutates(&c.handle(Input::Tick, t0 + Duration::from_secs(30))), [1]);
        assert_eq!(c.app.status.write_pause, None);
    }

        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let (mut c, t0) = opened(dir.path());
        submit(&mut c, set_status("a", "o_done"), t0);
        submit(&mut c, set_status("b", "o_done"), t0);
        done(&mut c, 1, Ok(Confirmed::Applied), t0);

        assert_eq!(status("a").as_deref(), Some("o_done"));

    /// Edits still in flight for the old board must not hold the new board's write slots,
    /// and the old board's queued edit is never sent.
    #[test]
    fn opening_another_board_forgets_queued_edits() {
        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let other: BoardRef = "tviles/9".parse().unwrap();
        let mut other_snap = snapshot();
        other_snap.project.board = other.clone();
        save_cache(&cache_path(dir.path(), &other), &other_snap).unwrap();

        let mut sent = Vec::new();

            sent.extend(mutates(&submit(&mut c, set_status(item, "o_done"), t0)));
        }
        assert_eq!(sent, [1, 2, 3, 4], "four in flight, the fifth queued");
        let old = c.generation();
        c.commands(vec![Command::PickBoard(other)], t0);
        assert!(mutates(&c.handle(Input::Tick, t0)).is_empty(), "the old board's fifth edit is never sent");
        let late = Input::Sync { generation: old, event: SyncEvent::OpDone { id: 1, result: Ok(Confirmed::Applied) } };
        assert!(c.handle(late, t0).is_empty(), "late results of the old board are dropped");
        assert_eq!(mutates(&submit(&mut c, set_status("a", "o_done"), t0)), [1], "the new board writes at once");
    }
```

```rust
                        .app
```

Add the dispatcher:

```rust
    /// Spawns every edit the queue lets go now, and mirrors the write pause into the header.
    fn dispatch(&mut self, now: Instant) -> Vec<Effect> {
        let mut effects = Vec::new();
        let project = self.app.project().map(|p| p.id.clone());
        for id in self.queue.ready(now) {
            let op = self
                .app
                .store
                .pending()
                .iter()
                .find(|p| p.id == id)
                .map(|p| p.op.clone());
            match (op, &project) {
                (Some(op), Some(project)) => effects.push(self.spawn(SyncJob::Mutate {
                    id,
                    project: project.clone(),
                    op,
                })),
                // Settled or gone meanwhile: nothing to send.
                _ => self.queue.finished(id),
            }
        }
        self.app.status.write_pause = self.queue.paused_until(now);
        effects
    }
```

```rust
            Input::Tick => {
                let mut effects = self
                    .scheduler
                    .tick(now)
                    .map(|k| self.poll(k, now))
                    .unwrap_or_default();
                effects.extend(self.dispatch(now));
                effects
            }
```

At the start of `on_sync` (before the rate handling):

```rust
        if let SyncEvent::OpDone { id, result } = &event {
            if let Err(GithubError::RateLimited { retry_after_secs }) = result {
                // Not a failure: the edit goes back to the front and writes pause.
                self.queue
                    .retry_after(*id, now + Duration::from_secs(*retry_after_secs));
                self.app.status.write_pause = self.queue.paused_until(now);
                return Vec::new();
            }
            self.queue.finished(*id);
        }
        let op_done = matches!(event, SyncEvent::OpDone { .. });
```

- [ ] **Step 2: `value_for_bucket` (`src/model/field.rs`, in `impl Field`)**

```rust
    /// The value that puts an item in the bucket `key` (an option or iteration id).
    pub fn value_for_bucket(&self, key: &str) -> Option<crate::model::FieldValue> {
        use crate::model::FieldValue;
        match &self.kind {
            FieldKind::SingleSelect { options } => {
                options.iter().find(|o| o.id.0 == key).map(|o| FieldValue::SingleSelect {
                    option_id: o.id.clone(),
                    name: o.name.clone(),
                })
            }
            FieldKind::Iteration { iterations, completed } => iterations
                .iter()
                .chain(completed)
                .find(|i| i.id.0 == key)
                .map(|i| FieldValue::Iteration {
                    iteration_id: i.id.clone(),
                    title: i.title.clone(),
                    start_date: i.start_date.clone(),
                }),
            _ => None,
        }
    }
```

```rust
    pub fn kinds(self) -> &'static [OpKind] {
        match self {
            Action::MovePrevColumn | Action::MoveNextColumn => &[OpKind::Move],
            Action::ReorderUp | Action::ReorderDown => &[OpKind::Reorder],
            _ => &[],
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Move and reorder (`src/ui/app/edit.rs`, in `impl App`)**

Add the import `use crate::ui::table::Row;` and:

```rust
    /// Runs an edit key on `action_item`. `None` when `action` is not an edit key.
    pub(crate) fn edit_key(&mut self, action: Action) -> Option<Vec<Command>> {
        let cmds = match action {
            Action::MovePrevColumn => self.move_card(-1),
            Action::MoveNextColumn => self.move_card(1),
            Action::ReorderUp => self.reorder(false),
            Action::ReorderDown => self.reorder(true),
            _ => return None,
        };
        Some(cmds)
    }

    /// `check`, plus what the current view rules out: moving needs a column field, and
    /// reordering needs an unsorted view (GitHub's rule). Every edit key, the action menu and
    /// the hints ask this, so the UI never offers what it would then refuse. The reason is
    /// the one-line message to show.
    pub(crate) fn check_here(&self, kind: OpKind, item: &Item) -> Result<(), String> {
        check(kind, item, &self.edit_context()).map_err(|d| self.denial_text(d))?;
        match kind {
            OpKind::Move if self.column_field().is_none() => {
                Err("This view has no single-select or iteration field to move along".into())
            }
            OpKind::Reorder => match self.current_view().and_then(|v| v.sort_by.first()) {
                Some(sort) => {
                    let name = self
                        .project()
                        .and_then(|p| p.field(&sort.field))
                        .map_or_else(|| "a field".to_string(), |f| f.name.clone());
                    Err(format!("This view is sorted by {name}; reordering needs an unsorted view"))
                }
                None => Ok(()),
            },
            _ => Ok(()),
        }
    }

    /// Moves the item to the neighbouring column among those the board shows (a column the
    /// filter hides is not shown, so it is skipped), purely by position (Decision 12, user-
    /// confirmed): never wraps, never clears. It stops at the first and the last option;
    /// "No <field>", drawn last, is never a destination, and from it `<` goes to the column
    /// before it. Clearing a field is `e`'s job. The table uses the same columns.
    fn move_card(&mut self, step: isize) -> Vec<Command> {
        let Some(item) = self.action_item().cloned() else {
            return Vec::new();
        };
        if let Err(reason) = self.check_here(OpKind::Move, &item) {
            self.status.flash = Some(reason);
            return Vec::new();
        }
        let Some(field) = self.column_field().cloned() else {
            return Vec::new();
        };
        let columns = self.board_columns();
        let Some(pos) = columns
            .iter()
            .position(|c| c.items().iter().any(|i| i.id == item.id))
        else {
            self.status.flash = Some("This item is not shown in this view".into());
            return Vec::new();
        };
        // `None`: past either edge, or the "No <field>" column (its key is `None`).
        let target: Option<String> = pos
            .checked_add_signed(step)
            .and_then(|i| columns.get(i))
            .and_then(|c| c.bucket.key.clone());
        let Some(key) = target else {
            self.status.flash = Some(
                if step < 0 {
                    "Already in the first column"
                } else {
                    "Already in the last column"
                }
                .into(),
            );
            return Vec::new();
        };
        let Some(value) = field.value_for_bucket(&key) else {
            return Vec::new();
        };
        self.submit(
            OpKind::Move,
            Op::SetField { item: item.id.clone(), field: field.id.clone(), value },
        )
    }

    /// Moves the item one place up or down among its neighbours: `K` puts it after its upper
    /// neighbour's predecessor in project order (first when there is none), `J` after its
    /// lower neighbour. Needs an unsorted view; stays inside the swimlane or group.
    fn reorder(&mut self, down: bool) -> Vec<Command> {
        let Some(item) = self.action_item().cloned() else {
            return Vec::new();
        };
        if let Err(reason) = self.check_here(OpKind::Reorder, &item) {
            self.status.flash = Some(reason);
            return Vec::new();
        }
        let Some(scope) = self.reorder_scope(&item.id) else {
            return Vec::new();
        };
        let Some(pos) = scope.iter().position(|i| *i == item.id) else {
            return Vec::new();
        };
        let neighbour = if down {
            scope.get(pos + 1)
        } else {
            pos.checked_sub(1).and_then(|p| scope.get(p))
        };
        let Some(neighbour) = neighbour.cloned() else {
            self.status.flash =
                Some(if down { "Already last here" } else { "Already first here" }.into());
            return Vec::new();
        };
        let after = if down {
            Some(neighbour)
        } else {
            self.predecessor(&neighbour, &item.id)
        };
        self.submit(OpKind::Reorder, Op::Reorder { item: item.id, after })
    }

    /// The ids `id` can be reordered among, in display order: its swimlane on a board; its
    /// group, or the whole table, otherwise.
    fn reorder_scope(&self, id: &ItemId) -> Option<Vec<ItemId>> {
        if self.layout().0 == Layout::Board {
            return self
                .board_columns()
                .iter()
                .flat_map(|c| c.lanes.iter())
                .find(|l| l.items.iter().any(|i| &i.id == id))
                .map(|l| l.items.iter().map(|i| i.id.clone()).collect());
        }
        let rows = self.table_rows();
        let at = rows.iter().position(|r| matches!(r, Row::Item(i) if &i.id == id))?;
        let start = rows[..at]
            .iter()
            .rposition(|r| matches!(r, Row::Group { .. }))
            .map_or(0, |g| g + 1);
        let end = rows[at..]
            .iter()
            .position(|r| matches!(r, Row::Group { .. }))
            .map_or(rows.len(), |g| at + g);
        Some(
            rows[start..end]
                .iter()
                .filter_map(|r| match r {
                    Row::Item(i) => Some(i.id.clone()),
                    Row::Group { .. } => None,
                })
                .collect(),
        )
    }

    /// The item before `of` in project order, skipping `skip` (the item being moved).
    fn predecessor(&self, of: &ItemId, skip: &ItemId) -> Option<ItemId> {
        let order = &self.snapshot()?.order;
        let at = order.iter().position(|i| i == of)?;
        order[..at].iter().rev().find(|i| *i != skip).cloned()
    }
```

- [ ] **Step 2: `still_matches` (`src/model/filter.rs`)**

```rust
use crate::model::item::Item;

/// Whether `item` still matches `filter`, as far as can be told without GitHub: every
/// single-select and iteration field the filter names allows the item's value, and an item
/// is archived exactly when the filter has `is:archived`. Qualifiers this cannot read count
/// as matching; GitHub's next list for the view decides those.
pub fn still_matches(filter: &str, item: &Item, fields: &[Field]) -> bool {
    let archived_view = tokens(filter)
        .iter()
        .any(|t| t.eq_ignore_ascii_case("is:archived"));
    if item.archived != archived_view {
        return false;
    }
    fields.iter().filter(|f| f.kind.can_be_column()).all(|f| {
        let constraint = column_constraint(filter, f);
        if !constraint.is_constrained() {
            return true;
        }
        let buckets = f.buckets().unwrap_or_default();
        let bucket = item
            .value(&f.id)
            .and_then(|v| v.bucket_key())
            .and_then(|key| buckets.iter().find(|b| b.matches(&key)).cloned());
        match bucket {
            Some(b) => constraint.allows(&b.title, false),
            None => constraint.allows("", true),
        }
    })
}
```

```rust
    pub fn view_items(&self) -> Option<Vec<&Item>> {
        let snap = self.snapshot()?;
        let view = self.current_view()?;
        let filter = self.effective_filter();
        let mut items: Vec<&Item> = if filter.trim().is_empty() {
            snap.all_items().into_iter().filter(|i| !i.archived).collect()
        } else {
            snap.view_items(&view.id)?
        };
        self.add_sticky(snap, &mut items);
        let query = self.search_query();
        if !query.is_empty() {
            items.retain(|i| search::matches(i, query));
        }
        sort_items(&mut items, &view.sort_by, &snap.project.fields);
        Some(items)
    }
```

and `board_columns` so excluded columns that hold items say so:

```rust
    pub fn board_columns(&self) -> Vec<Column<'_>> {
        let (Some(field), Some(view), Some(project)) =
            (self.column_field(), self.current_view(), self.project())
        else {
            return Vec::new();
        };
        let items = self.view_items().unwrap_or_default();
        let columns = build_columns(&items, field, view.group_field(&project.fields));
        let constraint = column_constraint(&self.effective_filter(), field);
        // A filter that allows no column at all was misread: `constrain_columns` shows every
        // column then, and none of them is "filtered".
        let reads = columns
            .iter()
            .any(|c| constraint.allows(&c.bucket.title, c.bucket.key.is_none()));
        constrain_columns(columns, &constraint)
            .into_iter()
            .map(|mut c| {
                // A column the filter rejects, kept for a card the user just edited into it
                // (spec: "Done (filtered)"). One kept for GitHub's own cards is not marked.
                if reads
                    && !constraint.allows(&c.bucket.title, c.bucket.key.is_none())
                    && c.items().iter().any(|i| self.is_sticky(&i.id))
                {
                    c.bucket.title = format!("{} (filtered)", c.bucket.title);
                }
                c
            })
            .collect()
    }
```

In `src/ui/app/edit.rs`:

```rust
use crate::model::{is_placeholder, still_matches};
use crate::store::BoardSnapshot;
use std::collections::HashMap;

/// A sticky item, and whether the view's list held it when it became sticky.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub(crate) struct Sticky {
    pub id: ItemId,
    pub listed: bool,
}
```

and in `impl App`:

```rust
    /// Keeps `id` in the current view for this visit, if it is shown there (or new).
    pub(crate) fn stick(&mut self, id: &ItemId) {
        if self.sticky.iter().any(|s| &s.id == id) {
            return;
        }
        let shown = self
            .view_items()
            .is_some_and(|items| items.iter().any(|i| &i.id == id));
        if !shown && !is_placeholder(id) {
            return;
        }
        let listed = match (self.snapshot(), self.current_view()) {
            (Some(s), Some(v)) => s.views.get(&v.id).is_some_and(|l| l.ids.contains(id)),
            _ => false,
        };
        self.sticky.push(Sticky { id: id.clone(), listed });
    }

    /// Puts each sticky item missing from `items` where its project rank places it.
    fn add_sticky<'a>(&'a self, snap: &'a BoardSnapshot, items: &mut Vec<&'a Item>) {
        if self.sticky.is_empty() {
            return;
        }
        let rank: HashMap<&ItemId, usize> =
            snap.order.iter().enumerate().map(|(i, id)| (id, i)).collect();
        let rank_of = |id: &ItemId| rank.get(id).copied().unwrap_or(usize::MAX);
        for sticky in &self.sticky {
            if items.iter().any(|i| i.id == sticky.id) {
                continue;
            }
            let Some(item) = snap.items.get(&sticky.id) else {
                continue;
            };
            let mine = rank_of(&sticky.id);
            let at = items
                .iter()
                .position(|i| rank_of(&i.id) > mine)
                .unwrap_or(items.len());
            items.insert(at, item);
        }
    }

    /// A sticky item the view no longer matches: GitHub's latest list for the view dropped
    /// it, or its values fail the filter as far as `still_matches` can tell.
    pub fn unmatched(&self, item: &Item) -> bool {
        let Some(sticky) = self.sticky.iter().find(|s| s.id == item.id) else {
            return false;
        };
        let (Some(snap), Some(view)) = (self.snapshot(), self.current_view()) else {
            return false;
        };
        let filter = self.effective_filter();
        let dropped = !filter.trim().is_empty()
            && sticky.listed
            && snap.views.get(&view.id).is_some_and(|l| !l.ids.contains(&item.id));
        dropped || !still_matches(&filter, item, &snap.project.fields)
    }
```

```rust
        let now = std::time::Instant::now();
        let mut a = app();
        assert!(write_flags(&a, now).is_empty());
        a.submit(
            OpKind::Move,
            Op::SetField {
                item: ItemId::new("a"),
                field: FieldId::new("F_status"),
                value: FieldValue::SingleSelect { option_id: OptionId::new("o_done"), name: "Done".into() },
            },
        );
        a.status.write_pause = Some(now + std::time::Duration::from_secs(30));
        assert_eq!(write_flags(&a, now), ["saving 1…", "writes paused 30s"]);
        a.on_sync(crate::sync::SyncEvent::Access { scopes: Some(vec!["repo".into()]) });
        assert!(write_flags(&a, now).contains(&"read-only: token lacks the project scope".to_string()));
        let screen = render_to_string(100, 12, |f| draw(f, &mut a));
        assert!(screen.lines().next().unwrap().contains("saving 1…"), "{screen}");
```

```rust
use crate::model::ItemId;
use std::collections::HashSet;

pub const PENDING_MARK: &str = "⋯";
pub const UNMATCHED_MARK: &str = "⊘";
pub const UNMATCHED_TEXT: &str = "⊘ no longer matches filter";

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default, PartialEq)]
pub struct ItemMarks {
    /// Items with edits still sending.
    pub pending: HashSet<ItemId>,
    /// Sticky items the view's filter no longer matches.
    pub unmatched: HashSet<ItemId>,
    /// A new item, highlighted until the next key press.
    pub highlight: Option<ItemId>,
}

impl ItemMarks {
    /// What goes before an item's title or reference: `⋯ ` while edits are sending, `⊘ `
    /// when it no longer matches the view.
    pub fn prefix(&self, id: &ItemId) -> String {
        let mut out = String::new();
        if self.pending.contains(id) {
            out.push_str(PENDING_MARK);
            out.push(' ');
        }
        if self.unmatched.contains(id) {
            out.push_str(UNMATCHED_MARK);
            out.push(' ');
        }
        out
    }
}
```

Add:

```rust
/// The header flags about writing: edits sending, a rate-limit pause, and why the board is
/// read-only.
pub fn write_flags(app: &App, now: std::time::Instant) -> Vec<String> {
    let mut flags = Vec::new();
    let pending = app.pending_count();
    if pending > 0 {
        flags.push(format!("saving {pending}…"));
    }
    if let Some(until) = app.status.write_pause {
        let left = until.saturating_duration_since(now).as_secs();
        if left > 0 {
            flags.push(format!("writes paused {left}s"));
        }
    }
    if app.project().is_some_and(|p| !p.viewer_can_update) {
        flags.push("read-only".into());
    } else if app.project().is_some() && !app.edit_context().has_project_scope() {
        flags.push("read-only: token lacks the project scope".into());
    }
    flags
}
```

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::ui::fixtures::{code, key, render_to_string};

    fn picker() -> ListPicker<u8> {
        ListPicker::new(
            "Status",
            vec![

                Choice::new("In Progress", 2).with_detail("current"),
                Choice::new("Done", 3),
            ],
        )

    #[test]
    fn fuzzy_matching_keeps_order_and_ignores_case_and_spaces() {
        assert!(fuzzy_match("inp", "In Progress"));
        assert!(fuzzy_match("in prog", "In Progress"));
        assert!(fuzzy_match("", "anything"));
        assert!(!fuzzy_match("pi", "In Progress"), "letters must come in order");
        assert!(fuzzy_match("bug", "🐛 Bug"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn typing_filters_and_enter_chooses() {
        let mut p = picker();
        for c in "dn".chars() {
            assert_eq!(p.handle(&key(c)), ListOutcome::None);
        }
        assert_eq!(p.visible().len(), 1);
        assert_eq!(p.handle(&code(KeyCode::Enter)), ListOutcome::Chosen(3));

    #[test]

        p.handle(&code(KeyCode::Down));
        p.handle(&code(KeyCode::Down));
        assert_eq!(p.current().unwrap().value, 3, "stops at the end");
        p.handle(&key('j'));
        assert_eq!(p.input, "j", "j is text in a typing picker");
        assert_eq!(p.handle(&code(KeyCode::Enter)), ListOutcome::None, "nothing matches");
        assert_eq!(p.handle(&code(KeyCode::Esc)), ListOutcome::Cancel);

        p.handle(&key('k'));
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Implement (top of `src/ui/popup.rs`)**

```rust
//! The popovers every editor shares: a list to pick one entry from (typing filters it) and
//! a one-line text input. Both are plain state machines; `render_*` draws them.

use crate::ui::text::{display_width, pad_to_width, truncate_to_width};
use crate::ui::theme::Theme;
use crossterm::event::{KeyCode, KeyEvent, KeyModifiers};
use ratatui::Frame;
use ratatui::layout::Rect;
use ratatui::text::{Line, Span};
use ratatui::widgets::{Block, Borders, Clear, Paragraph};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub struct Choice<T> {
    pub label: String,
    /// Dim text at the right: a key, "current", "completed".
    pub detail: String,
    pub value: T,
}

impl<T> Choice<T> {
    pub fn new(label: impl Into<String>, value: T) -> Self {
        Self { label: label.into(), detail: String::new(), value }
    }

    pub fn with_detail(mut self, detail: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        self.detail = detail.into();
        self
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub struct ListPicker<T> {
    pub title: String,
    pub choices: Vec<Choice<T>>,
    pub input: String,
    pub selected: usize,
    /// The choices are still loading.
    pub loading: bool,
    /// Why the choices could not load.
    pub error: Option<String>,
    /// Typing filters the list. Off in menus, whose letters are keys.
    pub typing: bool,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub enum ListOutcome<T> {
    None,
    Chosen(T),
    Cancel,
    /// A key a menu (`typing: false`) does not use itself.
    Key(KeyEvent),
}

/// Whether the characters of `query` (spaces ignored) appear in `text` in order, ignoring case.
pub fn fuzzy_match(query: &str, text: &str) -> bool {
    let mut hay = text.chars().flat_map(char::to_lowercase);
    query
        .chars()
        .filter(|c| !c.is_whitespace())
        .flat_map(char::to_lowercase)
        .all(|q| hay.any(|h| h == q))
}

fn plain_char(key: &KeyEvent) -> Option<char> {
    match key.code {
        KeyCode::Char(c) if !key.modifiers.intersects(KeyModifiers::CONTROL | KeyModifiers::ALT) => Some(c),
        _ => None,
    }
}

impl<T: Clone> ListPicker<T> {
    pub fn new(title: impl Into<String>, choices: Vec<Choice<T>>) -> Self {
        Self {
            title: title.into(),
            choices,
            input: String::new(),
            selected: 0,
            loading: false,
            error: None,
            typing: true,
        }
    }

    pub fn loading(title: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        let mut p = Self::new(title, Vec::new());
        p.loading = true;
        p
    }

    /// The choices arrived; what was typed meanwhile stays.
    pub fn set_choices(&mut self, choices: Vec<Choice<T>>) {
        self.choices = choices;
        self.loading = false;
        self.error = None;
        self.clamp();
    }

    pub fn visible(&self) -> Vec<&Choice<T>> {
        self.choices
            .iter()
            .filter(|c| !self.typing || fuzzy_match(&self.input, &c.label))
            .collect()
    }

    pub fn current(&self) -> Option<&Choice<T>> {
        self.visible().get(self.selected).copied()
    }

    pub fn handle(&mut self, key: &KeyEvent) -> ListOutcome<T> {
        match key.code {
            KeyCode::Esc => return ListOutcome::Cancel,
            KeyCode::Enter => {
                if let Some(c) = self.current() {
                    return ListOutcome::Chosen(c.value.clone());
                }
            }
            KeyCode::Up => self.selected = self.selected.saturating_sub(1),
            KeyCode::Down => self.selected += 1,
            KeyCode::Backspace if self.typing => {
                self.input.pop();
                self.selected = 0;
            }
            _ => match (plain_char(key), self.typing) {
                (Some(c), true) => {
                    self.input.push(c);
                    self.selected = 0;
                }
                (Some('j'), false) => self.selected += 1,
                (Some('k'), false) => self.selected = self.selected.saturating_sub(1),
                (_, false) => return ListOutcome::Key(*key),
                (None, true) => {}
            },
        }
        self.clamp();
        ListOutcome::None
    }

    fn clamp(&mut self) {
        self.selected = self.selected.min(self.visible().len().saturating_sub(1));
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum LineOutcome {
    Typing,
    Submit,
    Cancel,
}

/// One-line editing: characters append, Backspace deletes, Enter submits, Esc cancels.
pub fn edit_line(input: &mut String, key: &KeyEvent) -> LineOutcome {
    match key.code {
        KeyCode::Enter => LineOutcome::Submit,
        KeyCode::Esc => LineOutcome::Cancel,
        KeyCode::Backspace => {
            input.pop();
            LineOutcome::Typing
        }
        _ => {
            if let Some(c) = plain_char(key) {
                input.push(c);
            }
            LineOutcome::Typing
        }
    }
}

/// A `width`×`height` box centred in `area`, clamped to it.
pub fn centered(area: Rect, width: u16, height: u16) -> Rect {
    let w = width.min(area.width);
    let h = height.min(area.height);
    Rect {
        x: area.x + (area.width - w) / 2,
        y: area.y + (area.height - h) / 2,
        width: w,
        height: h,
    }
}

fn boxed(frame: &mut Frame, rect: Rect, title: &str, lines: Vec<Line<'static>>) {
    frame.render_widget(Clear, rect);
    frame.render_widget(
        Paragraph::new(lines).block(
            Block::default()
                .borders(Borders::ALL)
                .title(format!(" {} ", truncate_to_width(title, rect.width.saturating_sub(4) as usize))),
        ),
        rect,
    );
}

pub fn render_list_picker<T>(frame: &mut Frame, area: Rect, picker: &ListPicker<T>, theme: &Theme)
where
    T: Clone,
{
    let visible = picker.visible();
    let rows = visible.len().clamp(1, 15) as u16 + if picker.typing { 1 } else { 0 };
    let rect = centered(area, area.width.saturating_sub(4).min(64), rows + 2);
    let inner = rect.width.saturating_sub(2) as usize;
    let mut lines = Vec::new();
    if picker.typing {
        lines.push(Line::styled(format!("> {}", picker.input), theme.accent()));
    }
    if let Some(e) = &picker.error {
        lines.push(Line::styled(truncate_to_width(e, inner), theme.error()));
    } else if picker.loading {
        lines.push(Line::styled("Loading…", theme.dim()));
    } else if visible.is_empty() {
        lines.push(Line::styled("No matches", theme.dim()));
    }
    let room = (rect.height as usize).saturating_sub(2 + lines.len());
    let start = picker.selected.saturating_sub(room.saturating_sub(1));
    for (i, choice) in visible.iter().enumerate().skip(start).take(room) {
        let detail_w = display_width(&choice.detail);
        let label_w = inner.saturating_sub(detail_w + 1);
        let style = if i == picker.selected { theme.selected() } else { ratatui::style::Style::default() };
        lines.push(Line::from(vec![
            Span::styled(pad_to_width(&truncate_to_width(&choice.label, label_w), label_w), style),
            Span::styled(" ", style),
            Span::styled(truncate_to_width(&choice.detail, inner.saturating_sub(label_w + 1)), style.patch(theme.dim())),
        ]));
    }
    boxed(frame, rect, &picker.title, lines);
}

pub fn render_line_popup(
    frame: &mut Frame,
    area: Rect,
    title: &str,
    input: &str,
    hint: &str,
    error: Option<&str>,
    theme: &Theme,
) {
    let rect = centered(area, area.width.saturating_sub(4).min(64), 5);
    let inner = rect.width.saturating_sub(2) as usize;
    let lines = vec![
        Line::styled(format!("> {input}"), theme.accent()),
        Line::styled(truncate_to_width(hint, inner), theme.dim()),
        match error {
            Some(e) => Line::styled(truncate_to_width(e, inner), theme.error()),
            None => Line::raw(""),
        },
    ];
    boxed(frame, rect, title, lines);
}
```

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::ui::fixtures::{code, key, render_to_string};

    fn labels() -> Vec<MultiOption<&'static str>> {
        ["docs", "Good First Issue", "🐛 bug"]
            .into_iter()
            .map(|l| MultiOption { label: l.to_string(), color: Some("0075ca".into()), value: l })
            .collect()
    }

    #[test]
    fn prechecked_first_and_apply_sends_only_the_difference() {
        let mut m = MultiSelect::new("Labels", labels(), |v| *v == "🐛 bug");
        assert_eq!(m.options[0].value, "🐛 bug");
        assert_eq!(m.checked, [true, false, false]);
        m.handle(&key(' ')); // uncheck bug
        m.handle(&code(KeyCode::Down));
        m.handle(&key(' ')); // check docs
        match m.handle(&code(KeyCode::Enter)) {
            MultiOutcome::Apply { added, removed, chosen } => {
                assert_eq!((added, removed, chosen), (vec!["docs"], vec!["🐛 bug"], vec!["docs"]));
            }
            other => panic!("expected Apply, got {other:?}"),
        }
    }

    /// Review Focus 4.
    #[test]
    fn filter_is_case_insensitive_and_space_toggles() {
        let mut m = MultiSelect::new("Labels", labels(), |_| false);
        for c in "good".chars() {
            m.handle(&key(c));
        }
        assert_eq!(m.visible().len(), 1);
        m.handle(&key(' '));
        assert_eq!(m.input, "good", "space toggled, it did not type");
        let MultiOutcome::Apply { added, .. } = m.handle(&code(KeyCode::Enter)) else {
            panic!("expected Apply")
        };
        assert_eq!(added, ["Good First Issue"]);
        let mut e = MultiSelect::new("Labels", labels(), |_| false);
        for c in "BUG".chars() {
            e.handle(&key(c));
        }
        assert_eq!(e.visible().len(), 1, "matches 🐛 bug");
    }

    #[test]
    fn esc_cancels_and_an_unchanged_set_applies_nothing() {
        let mut m = MultiSelect::new("Labels", labels(), |v| *v == "docs");
        assert_eq!(m.handle(&code(KeyCode::Esc)), MultiOutcome::Cancel);
        let MultiOutcome::Apply { added, removed, .. } = m.handle(&code(KeyCode::Enter)) else {
            panic!("expected Apply")
        };
        assert!(added.is_empty() && removed.is_empty());
    }

    #[test]
    fn options_that_load_later_keep_the_typed_filter() {
        let mut m: MultiSelect<&str> = MultiSelect::loading("Assignees");
        m.handle(&key('d'));
        m.handle(&key('o'));
        assert_eq!(m.handle(&key(' ')), MultiOutcome::None, "nothing to toggle yet");
        m.set_options(labels(), |v| *v == "docs");
        assert!(!m.loading);
        assert_eq!(m.input, "do");
        assert_eq!(m.visible(), [0], "docs, checked, is first");
    }

    #[test]
    fn renders_checkboxes() {
        let m = MultiSelect::new("Labels", labels(), |v| *v == "docs");
        let screen = render_to_string(60, 10, |f| render_multiselect(f, f.area(), &m, &Theme::plain()));
        assert!(screen.contains("[x] docs") && screen.contains("[ ] Good First Issue"), "{screen}");
        assert!(screen.contains("space toggles"), "{screen}");
    }

    /// Spec section 9: pickers are snapshot-tested at 60, 100 and 200 columns.
    #[test]
    fn the_multiselect_at_three_widths() {
        let m = MultiSelect::new("Labels", labels(), |v| *v == "docs");
        for w in [60u16, 100, 200] {
            let screen = render_to_string(w, 12, |f| render_multiselect(f, f.area(), &m, &Theme::plain()));
            insta::assert_snapshot!(format!("multiselect_{w}"), screen);
        }
    }
}
```

```rust
//! The checkbox popover for assignees, labels and multi-select fields: type to filter,
//! ↑/↓ move, Space toggles, Enter applies (only what changed), Esc cancels.

use crate::ui::popup::{centered, fuzzy_match};
use crate::ui::text::{pad_to_width, truncate_to_width};
use crate::ui::theme::Theme;
use crossterm::event::{KeyCode, KeyEvent, KeyModifiers};
use ratatui::Frame;
use ratatui::layout::Rect;
use ratatui::style::Style;
use ratatui::text::{Line, Span};
use ratatui::widgets::{Block, Borders, Clear, Paragraph};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub struct MultiOption<T> {
    pub label: String,
    /// A GitHub label colour (hex), drawn as a pill.
    pub color: Option<String>,
    pub value: T,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub struct MultiSelect<T> {
    pub title: String,
    pub options: Vec<MultiOption<T>>,
    pub checked: Vec<bool>,
    initial: Vec<bool>,
    pub input: String,
    /// Index into `visible()`.
    pub selected: usize,
    pub loading: bool,
    pub error: Option<String>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub enum MultiOutcome<T> {
    None,
    /// Enter: what was checked and unchecked, and everything checked now.
    Apply { added: Vec<T>, removed: Vec<T>, chosen: Vec<T> },
    Cancel,
}

impl<T: Clone> MultiSelect<T> {
    /// The options in the order given, checked ones first.
    pub fn new(title: impl Into<String>, options: Vec<MultiOption<T>>, is_checked: impl Fn(&T) -> bool) -> Self {
        let (mut on, off): (Vec<_>, Vec<_>) = options.into_iter().partition(|o| is_checked(&o.value));
        let n = on.len();
        on.extend(off);
        let checked: Vec<bool> = (0..on.len()).map(|i| i < n).collect();
        Self {
            title: title.into(),
            options: on,
            initial: checked.clone(),
            checked,
            input: String::new(),
            selected: 0,
            loading: false,
            error: None,
        }
    }

    pub fn loading(title: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        let mut m = Self::new(title, Vec::new(), |_| false);
        m.loading = true;
        m
    }

    /// The options arrived; what was typed stays.
    pub fn set_options(&mut self, options: Vec<MultiOption<T>>, is_checked: impl Fn(&T) -> bool) {
        let input = std::mem::take(&mut self.input);
        *self = Self::new(std::mem::take(&mut self.title), options, is_checked);
        self.input = input;
        self.clamp();
    }

    /// Indexes of the options the typed text matches.
    pub fn visible(&self) -> Vec<usize> {
        (0..self.options.len())
            .filter(|&i| fuzzy_match(&self.input, &self.options[i].label))
            .collect()
    }

    pub fn handle(&mut self, key: &KeyEvent) -> MultiOutcome<T> {
        match key.code {
            KeyCode::Esc => return MultiOutcome::Cancel,
            KeyCode::Enter if !self.loading => return self.apply(),
            KeyCode::Up => self.selected = self.selected.saturating_sub(1),
            KeyCode::Down => self.selected += 1,
            KeyCode::Char(' ') => {
                if let Some(&i) = self.visible().get(self.selected) {
                    self.checked[i] = !self.checked[i];
                }
            }
            KeyCode::Backspace => {
                self.input.pop();
                self.selected = 0;
            }
            KeyCode::Char(c) if !key.modifiers.intersects(KeyModifiers::CONTROL | KeyModifiers::ALT) => {
                self.input.push(c);
                self.selected = 0;
            }
            _ => {}
        }
        self.clamp();
        MultiOutcome::None
    }

    fn apply(&self) -> MultiOutcome<T> {
        let pick = |f: &dyn Fn(usize) -> bool| -> Vec<T> {
            (0..self.options.len()).filter(|&i| f(i)).map(|i| self.options[i].value.clone()).collect()
        };
        MultiOutcome::Apply {
            added: pick(&|i| self.checked[i] && !self.initial[i]),
            removed: pick(&|i| !self.checked[i] && self.initial[i]),
            chosen: pick(&|i| self.checked[i]),
        }
    }

    fn clamp(&mut self) {
        self.selected = self.selected.min(self.visible().len().saturating_sub(1));
    }
}

pub fn render_multiselect<T: Clone>(frame: &mut Frame, area: Rect, m: &MultiSelect<T>, theme: &Theme) {
    let visible = m.visible();
    let rows = visible.len().clamp(1, 15) as u16 + 2;
    let rect = centered(area, area.width.saturating_sub(4).min(64), rows + 2);
    let inner = rect.width.saturating_sub(2) as usize;
    let mut lines = vec![Line::styled(format!("> {}", m.input), theme.accent())];
    if let Some(e) = &m.error {
        lines.push(Line::styled(truncate_to_width(e, inner), theme.error()));
    } else if m.loading {
        lines.push(Line::styled("Loading…", theme.dim()));
    } else if visible.is_empty() {
        lines.push(Line::styled("No matches", theme.dim()));
    }
    let room = (rect.height as usize).saturating_sub(3 + lines.len());
    let start = m.selected.saturating_sub(room.saturating_sub(1));
    for (row, &i) in visible.iter().enumerate().skip(start).take(room) {
        let option = &m.options[i];
        let style = if row == m.selected { theme.selected() } else { Style::default() };
        let mark = if m.checked[i] { "[x] " } else { "[ ] " };
        let label = truncate_to_width(&option.label, inner.saturating_sub(4));
        let label_style = match (&option.color, theme.color) {
            (Some(hex), true) => theme.label(hex),
            _ => style,
        };
        let pad = inner.saturating_sub(4 + crate::ui::text::display_width(&label));
        lines.push(Line::from(vec![
            Span::styled(mark, style),
            Span::styled(label, label_style),
            Span::styled(" ".repeat(pad), style),
        ]));
    }
    lines.push(Line::styled(
        pad_to_width("space toggles · enter applies · esc cancels", inner),
        theme.dim(),
    ));
    frame.render_widget(Clear, rect);
    frame.render_widget(
        Paragraph::new(lines).block(Block::default().borders(Borders::ALL).title(format!(" {} ", m.title))),
        rect,
    );
}
```

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
```

```rust
//! What a typed date or number means (spec section 6, Editors).

use time::{Date, Duration, Month, Weekday};

/// `YYYY-MM-DD`, when it names a real day.
pub fn parse_iso_date(text: &str) -> Option<Date> {
    let (y, rest) = text.split_once('-')?;
    let (m, d) = rest.split_once('-')?;
    let month = Month::try_from(m.parse::<u8>().ok()?).ok()?;
    Date::from_calendar_date(y.parse().ok()?, month, d.parse().ok()?).ok()
}

pub fn format_iso_date(date: Date) -> String {
    format!("{:04}-{:02}-{:02}", date.year(), u8::from(date.month()), date.day())
}

fn weekday(word: &str) -> Option<Weekday> {
    Some(match word {
        "mon" | "monday" => Weekday::Monday,
        "tue" | "tues" | "tuesday" => Weekday::Tuesday,
        "wed" | "wednesday" => Weekday::Wednesday,
        "thu" | "thur" | "thurs" | "thursday" => Weekday::Thursday,
        "fri" | "friday" => Weekday::Friday,
        "sat" | "saturday" => Weekday::Saturday,
        "sun" | "sunday" => Weekday::Sunday,
        _ => return None,
    })
}

/// `+3d`, `-2d`, `+1w`, `+3` (days).
fn offset(text: &str, today: Date) -> Option<Date> {
    let (sign, rest) = match text.as_bytes().first()? {
        b'+' => (1, &text[1..]),
        b'-' => (-1, &text[1..]),
        _ => return None,
    };
    let (number, days) = if let Some(n) = rest.strip_suffix('w') {
        (n, 7)
    } else {
        (rest.strip_suffix('d').unwrap_or(rest), 1)
    };
    let n: i64 = number.parse().ok()?;
    today.checked_add(Duration::days(sign * n * days))
}

/// A typed date: `2026-10-03`, `today`, `tomorrow`, `+3d`, `-2d`, `+1w`, or a weekday name
/// (`fri`, `friday`) meaning the next such day after today. Empty means "clear the field".
pub fn parse_date_input(text: &str, today: Date) -> Result<Option<Date>, String> {
    let t = text.trim().to_lowercase();
    if t.is_empty() {
        return Ok(None);
    }
    let found = match t.as_str() {
        "today" => Some(today),
        "tomorrow" => today.next_day(),
        _ => parse_iso_date(&t).or_else(|| offset(&t, today)).or_else(|| {
            let w = weekday(&t)?;
            let mut d = today.next_day()?;
            while d.weekday() != w {
                d = d.next_day()?;
            }
            Some(d)
        }),
    };
    found
        .map(Some)
        .ok_or_else(|| format!("`{}` is not a date: try 2026-10-03, +3d or fri", text.trim()))
}

/// A typed number; empty means "clear the field".
pub fn parse_number_input(text: &str) -> Result<Option<f64>, String> {
    let t = text.trim();
    if t.is_empty() {
        return Ok(None);
    }
    match t.parse::<f64>() {
        Ok(n) if n.is_finite() => Ok(Some(n)),
        _ => Err(format!("`{t}` is not a number")),
    }
}
```

```rust
//! The popovers: what each one edits and what its keys do.

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::ui::app::test_app;
    use crate::ui::fixtures::{code, key};
    use time::macros::date;

    fn type_text(a: &mut App, text: &str) {
        for c in text.chars() {
            a.handle_key(key(c));
        }
    }

    fn value(a: &App, item: &str, field: &str) -> Option<FieldValue> {
        a.snapshot().unwrap().items[&ItemId::new(item)].value(&FieldId::new(field)).cloned()
    }

    fn field_labels(a: &App) -> Vec<String> {
        match &a.popup {
            Some(Popup::Fields { picker, .. }) => picker.visible().iter().map(|c| c.label.clone()).collect(),
            other => panic!("expected the field menu, got {other:?}"),
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn e_lists_the_editable_fields_with_current_values() {
        let mut a = test_app();
        assert!(a.handle_key(key('e')).is_empty());
        assert_eq!(a.mode, Mode::Popup);
        assert_eq!(field_labels(&a), ["Status", "Priority", "Notes", "Size", "Due", "Sprint", "Areas"]);
        let Some(Popup::Fields { picker, .. }) = &a.popup else { unreachable!() };
        assert_eq!(picker.choices[0].detail, "Todo");
    }

    #[test]
    fn choosing_an_option_sets_it() {
        let mut a = test_app();
        a.handle_key(key('e'));
        type_text(&mut a, "sta");
        a.handle_key(code(KeyCode::Enter));
        assert!(matches!(a.popup, Some(Popup::Value { .. })));
        type_text(&mut a, "done");
        assert_eq!(a.handle_key(code(KeyCode::Enter)), vec![Command::Send(1)]);
        assert_eq!(a.mode, Mode::Normal);
        assert_eq!(value(&a, "a", "F_status").map(|v| v.display()).as_deref(), Some("Done"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn clear_is_offered_when_the_item_has_a_value() {
        let mut a = test_app();
        a.handle_key(key('e'));
        a.handle_key(code(KeyCode::Enter)); // Status
        type_text(&mut a, "clear");
        assert_eq!(a.handle_key(code(KeyCode::Enter)), vec![Command::Send(1)]);
        assert_eq!(value(&a, "a", "F_status"), None);
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_bad_date_keeps_the_input_open_with_the_reason() {
        let mut a = test_app();
        a.handle_key(key('e'));
        type_text(&mut a, "due");
        a.handle_key(code(KeyCode::Enter));
        type_text(&mut a, "soon");
        assert!(a.handle_key(code(KeyCode::Enter)).is_empty());
        let Some(Popup::Line { error, .. }) = &a.popup else { panic!("still the date input") };
        assert!(error.as_deref().unwrap().contains("not a date"));
        for _ in 0..4 {
            a.handle_key(code(KeyCode::Backspace));
        }
        type_text(&mut a, "2026-10-03");
        assert_eq!(a.handle_key(code(KeyCode::Enter)), vec![Command::Send(1)]);
        assert_eq!(value(&a, "a", "F_due"), Some(FieldValue::Date("2026-10-03".into())));
    }

    #[test]
    fn text_and_numbers_are_set_and_an_empty_input_clears() {
        let mut a = test_app();
        a.handle_key(key('e'));
        type_text(&mut a, "notes");
        a.handle_key(code(KeyCode::Enter));
        type_text(&mut a, "Needs a repro");
        a.handle_key(code(KeyCode::Enter));
        assert_eq!(value(&a, "a", "F_notes"), Some(FieldValue::Text("Needs a repro".into())));
        a.handle_key(key('e'));
        type_text(&mut a, "size");
        a.handle_key(code(KeyCode::Enter));
        type_text(&mut a, "2.5");
        a.handle_key(code(KeyCode::Enter));
        assert_eq!(value(&a, "a", "F_size"), Some(FieldValue::Number(2.5)));
        a.handle_key(key('e'));
        type_text(&mut a, "notes");
        a.handle_key(code(KeyCode::Enter));
        let Some(Popup::Line { input, .. }) = &a.popup else { panic!("text input") };
        assert_eq!(input, "Needs a repro", "pre-filled");
        for _ in 0.."Needs a repro".len() {
            a.handle_key(code(KeyCode::Backspace));
        }
        a.handle_key(code(KeyCode::Enter));
        assert_eq!(value(&a, "a", "F_notes"), None);
        assert_eq!(a.pending_count(), 3);
    }

    #[test]
    fn multi_select_applies_the_new_set() {
        let mut a = test_app();
        a.handle_key(key('e'));
        type_text(&mut a, "areas");
        a.handle_key(code(KeyCode::Enter));
        a.handle_key(key(' '));
        assert_eq!(a.handle_key(code(KeyCode::Enter)), vec![Command::Send(1)]);
        assert_eq!(
            value(&a, "a", "F_areas"),
            Some(FieldValue::MultiSelect { option_ids: vec![OptionId::new("m_api")], names: vec!["api".into()] })
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn iteration_choices_put_the_current_one_first() {
        let p = crate::ui::fixtures::project();
        let sprint = p.fields.iter().find(|f| f.name == "Sprint").unwrap();
        let current = FieldValue::Iteration {
            iteration_id: IterationId::new("it3"),
            title: "Sprint 3".into(),
            start_date: "2026-10-15".into(),
        };
        let choices = iteration_choices(sprint, Some(&current), date!(2026 - 10 - 12));
        let rows: Vec<(&str, &str)> = choices.iter().map(|c| (c.label.as_str(), c.detail.as_str())).collect();
        assert_eq!(
            rows,
            [("Sprint 2", "current"), ("Sprint 3", "✓"), ("Sprint 1", "completed"), ("Clear Sprint", "")]
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn esc_closes_the_popover_and_returns_to_the_detail() {
        let mut a = test_app();
        a.handle_key(code(KeyCode::Enter));
        a.handle_key(key('e'));
        assert_eq!(a.mode, Mode::Popup);
        a.handle_key(code(KeyCode::Esc));
        assert_eq!(a.mode, Mode::Detail);
        assert!(a.popup.is_none());
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_private_item_says_why_instead_of_opening() {
        let mut a = test_app();
        let mut snap = a.snapshot().unwrap().clone();
        snap.items.get_mut(&ItemId::new("a")).unwrap().content = crate::model::ItemContent::Redacted;
        a.store.apply(crate::store::StoreUpdate::Replace(snap));
        assert!(a.handle_key(key('e')).is_empty());
        assert_eq!(a.mode, Mode::Normal);
        assert!(a.status.flash.as_deref().unwrap().starts_with("You can't see"));
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the popovers (top of `src/ui/app/popups.rs`)**

```rust
use super::*;
use crate::model::{
    FieldValue, Iteration, OptionId, check, format_iso_date, parse_date_input, parse_iso_date,
    parse_number_input,
};
use crate::ops::Op;
use crate::ui::multiselect::{MultiOption, MultiOutcome, MultiSelect};
use crate::ui::popup::{Choice, LineOutcome, ListOutcome, ListPicker, edit_line};
use crate::ui::text::truncate_to_width;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum LineKind {
    Text,
    Number,
    Date,
}

/// What the `e` menu can open.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub enum FieldTarget {
    Field(FieldId),
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub enum Popup {
    /// `e`: which field of `item` to edit.
    Fields { item: ItemId, picker: ListPicker<FieldTarget> },
    /// A single-select or iteration value; `None` clears it.
    Value { item: ItemId, field: FieldId, picker: ListPicker<Option<FieldValue>> },
    /// A multi-select field.
    Options { item: ItemId, field: FieldId, select: MultiSelect<OptionId> },
    /// A text, number or date field.
    Line { item: ItemId, field: FieldId, kind: LineKind, title: String, input: String, error: Option<String> },
}

/// The date "today" means. UTC: a typed `+1d` near midnight may be a day off locally.
fn today() -> time::Date {
    time::OffsetDateTime::now_utc().date()
}

fn editable(kind: &FieldKind) -> bool {
    matches!(
        kind,
        FieldKind::Text
            | FieldKind::Number
            | FieldKind::Date
            | FieldKind::SingleSelect { .. }
            | FieldKind::MultiSelect { .. }
            | FieldKind::Iteration { .. }
    )
}

/// The iterations to pick from: the current one, then the others by start date, then the
/// completed ones newest first; the item's own value is marked `✓`, and "Clear" comes last
/// when there is a value to clear.
pub(crate) fn iteration_choices(
    field: &Field,
    current: Option<&FieldValue>,
    today: time::Date,
) -> Vec<Choice<Option<FieldValue>>> {
    let FieldKind::Iteration { iterations, completed } = &field.kind else {
        return Vec::new();
    };
    let set = current.and_then(|v| v.bucket_key());
    let is_now = |i: &Iteration| {
        parse_iso_date(&i.start_date).is_some_and(|start| {
            start <= today && today < start + time::Duration::days(i.duration_days.into())
        })
    };
    let mut open: Vec<&Iteration> = iterations.iter().collect();
    open.sort_by(|a, b| a.start_date.cmp(&b.start_date));
    let (now, later): (Vec<&Iteration>, Vec<&Iteration>) = open.into_iter().partition(|i| is_now(i));
    let mut done: Vec<&Iteration> = completed.iter().collect();
    done.sort_by(|a, b| b.start_date.cmp(&a.start_date));
    let choice = |i: &Iteration, detail: &str| {
        let detail = if set.as_deref() == Some(i.id.as_str()) { "✓".to_string() } else { detail.to_string() };
        Choice::new(
            i.title.clone(),
            Some(FieldValue::Iteration {
                iteration_id: i.id.clone(),
                title: i.title.clone(),
                start_date: i.start_date.clone(),
            }),
        )
        .with_detail(detail)
    };
    let mut out: Vec<_> = now.into_iter().map(|i| choice(i, "current")).collect();
    out.extend(later.into_iter().map(|i| choice(i, "")));
    out.extend(done.into_iter().map(|i| choice(i, "completed")));
    if current.is_some() {
        out.push(Choice::new(format!("Clear {}", field.name), None));
    }
    out
}

/// What a submitted line means for a field of `kind`: `Ok(None)` clears it.
fn line_value(kind: LineKind, input: &str) -> Result<Option<FieldValue>, String> {
    match kind {
        LineKind::Text => {
            let t = input.trim();
            Ok((!t.is_empty()).then(|| FieldValue::Text(t.to_string())))
        }
        LineKind::Number => parse_number_input(input).map(|n| n.map(FieldValue::Number)),
        LineKind::Date => parse_date_input(input, today())
            .map(|d| d.map(|d| FieldValue::Date(format_iso_date(d)))),
    }
}

impl App {
    pub(crate) fn open_popup(&mut self, popup: Popup) {
        self.popup = Some(popup);
        self.mode = Mode::Popup;
    }

    pub(crate) fn close_popup(&mut self) {
        self.popup = None;
        self.mode = self.base_mode();
    }

    /// `e`: the menu of the item's editable fields, each with its current value.
    pub(crate) fn open_field_menu(&mut self) -> Vec<Command> {
        let Some(item) = self.action_item().cloned() else {
            return Vec::new();
        };
        if let Err(denial) = check(OpKind::SetField, &item, &self.edit_context()) {
            self.status.flash = Some(self.denial_text(denial));
            return Vec::new();
        }
        let Some(project) = self.project() else {
            return Vec::new();
        };
        let choices: Vec<Choice<FieldTarget>> = project
            .fields
            .iter()
            .filter(|f| editable(&f.kind))
            .map(|f| {
                Choice::new(f.name.clone(), FieldTarget::Field(f.id.clone()))
                    .with_detail(item.field_value(f).map(|v| v.display()).unwrap_or_default())
            })
            .collect();
        if choices.is_empty() {
            self.status.flash = Some("This project has no fields to edit here".into());
            return Vec::new();
        }
        let title = format!("Edit · {}", truncate_to_width(item.title(), 40));
        self.open_popup(Popup::Fields { item: item.id, picker: ListPicker::new(title, choices) });
        Vec::new()
    }

    /// The editor for `field` of `item`, by field type.
    fn open_field_editor(&mut self, item: &ItemId, field: &FieldId) -> Vec<Command> {
        let found = self.project().and_then(|p| p.field(field).cloned()).zip(
            self.snapshot().and_then(|s| s.items.get(item)).cloned(),
        );
        let Some((f, it)) = found else {
            self.close_popup();
            return Vec::new();
        };
        let current = it.value(&f.id).cloned();
        let title = format!("{} · {}", f.name, truncate_to_width(it.title(), 40));
        let (item, field) = (item.clone(), f.id.clone());
        let line = |kind: LineKind, input: String| Popup::Line {
            item: item.clone(),
            field: field.clone(),
            kind,
            title: title.clone(),
            input,
            error: None,
        };
        let popup = match &f.kind {
            FieldKind::SingleSelect { options } => {
                let set = current.as_ref().and_then(|v| v.bucket_key());
                let mut choices: Vec<Choice<Option<FieldValue>>> = options
                    .iter()
                    .map(|o| {
                        let mark = if set.as_deref() == Some(o.id.as_str()) { "✓" } else { "" };
                        Choice::new(
                            o.name.clone(),
                            Some(FieldValue::SingleSelect { option_id: o.id.clone(), name: o.name.clone() }),
                        )
                        .with_detail(mark)
                    })
                    .collect();
                if current.is_some() {
                    choices.push(Choice::new(format!("Clear {}", f.name), None));
                }
                Popup::Value { item, field, picker: ListPicker::new(title, choices) }
            }
            FieldKind::Iteration { .. } => {
                let choices = iteration_choices(&f, current.as_ref(), today());
                Popup::Value { item, field, picker: ListPicker::new(title, choices) }
            }
            FieldKind::MultiSelect { options } => {
                let chosen: Vec<OptionId> = match &current {
                    Some(FieldValue::MultiSelect { option_ids, .. }) => option_ids.clone(),
                    _ => Vec::new(),
                };
                let options = options
                    .iter()
                    .map(|o| MultiOption { label: o.name.clone(), color: None, value: o.id.clone() })
                    .collect();
                Popup::Options { item, field, select: MultiSelect::new(title, options, |id| chosen.contains(id)) }
            }
            FieldKind::Text => line(LineKind::Text, current.map(|v| v.display()).unwrap_or_default()),
            FieldKind::Number => line(LineKind::Number, current.map(|v| v.display()).unwrap_or_default()),
            FieldKind::Date => line(LineKind::Date, current.map(|v| v.display()).unwrap_or_default()),
            _ => {
                self.close_popup();
                self.status.flash = Some(format!("{} can't be edited here", f.name));
                return Vec::new();
            }
        };
        self.open_popup(popup);
        Vec::new()
    }

    /// Sets or clears a field from a popover; nothing when clearing an empty field.
    fn submit_value(&mut self, item: ItemId, field: FieldId, value: Option<FieldValue>) -> Vec<Command> {
        self.close_popup();
        match value {
            Some(value) => self.submit(OpKind::SetField, Op::SetField { item, field, value }),
            None => {
                let has_value = self
                    .snapshot()
                    .and_then(|s| s.items.get(&item))
                    .is_some_and(|i| i.value(&field).is_some());
                if has_value {
                    self.submit(OpKind::SetField, Op::ClearField { item, field })
                } else {
                    Vec::new()
                }
            }
        }
    }

    pub(crate) fn handle_popup_key(&mut self, key: KeyEvent) -> Vec<Command> {
        let Some(mut popup) = self.popup.take() else {
            self.mode = self.base_mode();
            return Vec::new();
        };
        match &mut popup {
            Popup::Fields { item, picker } => match picker.handle(&key) {
                ListOutcome::Chosen(FieldTarget::Field(field)) => {
                    let item = item.clone();
                    return self.open_field_editor(&item, &field);
                }
                ListOutcome::Cancel => {
                    self.close_popup();
                    return Vec::new();
                }
                ListOutcome::None | ListOutcome::Key(_) => {}
            },
            Popup::Value { item, field, picker } => match picker.handle(&key) {
                ListOutcome::Chosen(value) => {
                    let (item, field) = (item.clone(), field.clone());
                    return self.submit_value(item, field, value);
                }
                ListOutcome::Cancel => {
                    self.close_popup();
                    return Vec::new();
                }
                ListOutcome::None | ListOutcome::Key(_) => {}
            },
            Popup::Options { item, field, select } => match select.handle(&key) {
                MultiOutcome::Apply { added, removed, chosen } => {
                    let (item, field) = (item.clone(), field.clone());
                    if added.is_empty() && removed.is_empty() {
                        self.close_popup();
                        return Vec::new();
                    }
                    let names = self.option_names(&field, &chosen);
                    let value = (!chosen.is_empty())
                        .then(|| FieldValue::MultiSelect { option_ids: chosen, names });
                    return self.submit_value(item, field, value);
                }
                MultiOutcome::Cancel => {
                    self.close_popup();
                    return Vec::new();
                }
                MultiOutcome::None => {}
            },
            Popup::Line { item, field, kind, input, error, .. } => match edit_line(input, &key) {
                LineOutcome::Submit => match line_value(*kind, input) {
                    Ok(value) => {
                        let (item, field) = (item.clone(), field.clone());
                        return self.submit_value(item, field, value);
                    }
                    Err(e) => *error = Some(e),
                },
                LineOutcome::Cancel => {
                    self.close_popup();
                    return Vec::new();
                }
                LineOutcome::Typing => *error = None,
            },
        }
        self.popup = Some(popup);
        Vec::new()
    }

    /// The names of multi-select options, in the order given.
    fn option_names(&self, field: &FieldId, ids: &[OptionId]) -> Vec<String> {
        let Some(FieldKind::MultiSelect { options }) = self.project().and_then(|p| p.field(field)).map(|f| &f.kind)
        else {
            return Vec::new();
        };
        ids.iter()
            .filter_map(|id| options.iter().find(|o| &o.id == id).map(|o| o.name.clone()))
            .collect()
    }
}
```

```rust
use crate::ui::app::Popup;
use crate::ui::multiselect::render_multiselect;
use crate::ui::popup::{render_line_popup, render_list_picker};

fn draw_popup(frame: &mut Frame, area: Rect, app: &App) {
    let Some(popup) = &app.popup else { return };
    let theme = &app.theme;
    match popup {
        Popup::Fields { picker, .. } => render_list_picker(frame, area, picker, theme),
        Popup::Value { picker, .. } => render_list_picker(frame, area, picker, theme),
        Popup::Options { select, .. } => render_multiselect(frame, area, select, theme),
        Popup::Line { kind, title, input, error, .. } => {
            let hint = match kind {
                crate::ui::app::LineKind::Text => "enter saves · empty clears · esc cancels",
                crate::ui::app::LineKind::Number => "a number · empty clears · esc cancels",
                crate::ui::app::LineKind::Date => "2026-10-03, +3d, fri · empty clears · esc cancels",
            };
            render_line_popup(frame, area, title, input, hint, error.as_deref(), theme)
        }
    }
}
```

### Task 18: The action menu (`Space`) and the edit-key hints

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/ui/keymap.rs`, `src/ui/app.rs`, `src/ui/app/edit.rs`, `src/ui/app/popups.rs`, `src/ui/chrome.rs`, `src/ui/snapshots/project_boards__ui__chrome__tests__full_screen_has_header_tabs_body_and_hints.snap`, `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-30-project-boards-design.md` (section 6, Keys)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `capabilities`, `check` (Task 3); `Popup`, `ListPicker` (Tasks 14, 17); `App::edit_key` (Task 11).
- Produces:
  - `Action::ActionMenu` (`action_menu`, key `space`); `Action::ALL` has 28 entries.
  - `Popup::Actions { item: ItemId, picker: ListPicker<MenuEntry> }` and `ui::app::MenuEntry::Key(Action)` (Part B adds entries without keys).
  - `edit::EDIT_ACTIONS: &[Action]` (the order of the menu and hints); `App::allowed_kind(&self, action: Action, item: &Item) -> Option<OpKind>`; `App::open_action_menu(&mut self) -> Vec<Command>`; `App::run_action(&mut self, action: Action) -> Vec<Command>`; `App::open_in_browser(&self) -> Vec<Command>`; `App::edit_hints(&self) -> Option<String>` (e.g. `e field · < > move · J K reorder · space actions`; Part B puts `a assignees · t labels` and the other single-key edits first).
  - **E17 (controller ruling, supersedes the `edit_hints` signature above):** `App::edit_hints(&self) -> Option<Vec<String>>` returns the edit entries without `space actions`; `App::actions_hint(&self) -> Option<String>` returns `space actions` when the item allows any action. The status line is `[notes] · edit hints · general hints · space actions · ? help`; `space actions` and `? help` are never dropped, and when room is short the edit hints go first from the end (`J K reorder`, `< > move`, `c comment`, ...), then the general hints (`chrome::status_hints`). The `space actions` text is no longer last in `edit_hints`; the `ends_with("space actions")` asserts below become `actions_hint() == Some("space actions")`.
  - The status line shows the edit hints of the selected (or open) item before the general hints, and always ends with `? help`: `chrome::status_line(left: &str, width: usize) -> String` cuts `left` with `…` to keep it (pre-flight ruling 4).

```rust
    fn menu(a: &App) -> Vec<(String, String)> {
        match &a.popup {
            Some(Popup::Actions { picker, .. }) => {
                picker.choices.iter().map(|c| (c.label.clone(), c.detail.clone())).collect()
            }
            other => panic!("expected the action menu, got {other:?}"),
        }

    #[test]
    fn space_lists_every_operation_the_item_allows_with_its_key() {

        let rows = menu(&a);
        // Later tasks add entries; these must stay, the moves first.
        assert_eq!(rows[0], ("Move to the previous column".to_string(), "<".to_string()));

            ("Move to the next column", ">"),
```

```rust
/// An entry of the action menu.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub enum MenuEntry {
    /// Runs the action, as its key would.
    Key(Action),
}
```

and in `impl App`:

```rust
    /// Space: every operation `action_item` allows, each with its key; or why there are none.
    pub(crate) fn open_action_menu(&mut self) -> Vec<Command> {
        let Some(item) = self.action_item().cloned() else {
            return Vec::new();
        };
        let key = |a: Action| self.keymap.keys_for(a).first().map(|k| k.label()).unwrap_or_default();
        let mut choices: Vec<Choice<MenuEntry>> = Vec::new();
        for &action in edit::EDIT_ACTIONS {
            if let Some(kind) = self.allowed_kind(action, &item) {
                choices.push(Choice::new(edit::menu_label(action, kind), MenuEntry::Key(action)).with_detail(key(action)));
            }
        }
        if item.url().is_some() && !choices.is_empty() {
            choices.push(Choice::new("Open in the browser", MenuEntry::Key(Action::OpenBrowser)).with_detail(key(Action::OpenBrowser)));
        }
        if choices.is_empty() {
            let denial = check(OpKind::Move, &item, &self.edit_context()).err();
            self.status.flash = Some(denial.map_or_else(|| "Nothing to do with this item".into(), |d| self.denial_text(d)));
            return Vec::new();
        }
        let mut picker = ListPicker::new(format!("Actions · {}", truncate_to_width(item.title(), 40)), choices);
        picker.typing = false;
        self.open_popup(Popup::Actions { item: item.id, picker });
        Vec::new()
    }

    fn run_menu_entry(&mut self, entry: MenuEntry) -> Vec<Command> {
        match entry {
            MenuEntry::Key(action) => self.run_action(action),
        }
    }
```
