- [ ] **Step 4: Hints and shared helpers (`src/ui/app/edit.rs`)**

```rust
/// The edit keys in the order the action menu and the hints list them.
pub(crate) const EDIT_ACTIONS: &[Action] = &[
    Action::MovePrevColumn,
    Action::MoveNextColumn,
    Action::ReorderUp,
    Action::ReorderDown,
    Action::EditField,
];

/// The menu's words for an action.
pub(crate) fn menu_label(action: Action, kind: OpKind) -> String {
    match action {
        Action::MovePrevColumn => "Move to the previous column".into(),
        Action::MoveNextColumn => "Move to the next column".into(),
        Action::ReorderUp => "Move up".into(),
        Action::ReorderDown => "Move down".into(),
        Action::EditField => "Edit a field".into(),
        _ => {
            let label = kind.label();
            let mut chars = label.chars();
            chars
                .next()
                .map(|first| first.to_uppercase().chain(chars).collect())
                .unwrap_or_default()
        }
    }
}

/// The hints' word for an action's key, e.g. `e field`.
fn hint_word(action: Action, kind: OpKind) -> &'static str {
    match action {
        Action::EditField => "field",
        _ => kind.label(),
    }
}

/// The single-action keys the hints list after move and reorder.
const HINTED: &[Action] = &[Action::EditField];
```

and in `impl App`:

```rust
    /// The first operation of `action` allowed on `item` here (`check_here`: the matrix and
    /// the view's rules).
    pub(crate) fn allowed_kind(&self, action: Action, item: &Item) -> Option<OpKind> {
        action.kinds().iter().copied().find(|k| self.check_here(*k, item).is_ok())
    }

    /// Runs `action` as its key would (the action menu uses this).
    pub(crate) fn run_action(&mut self, action: Action) -> Vec<Command> {
        if let Some(cmds) = self.edit_key(action) {
            return cmds;
        }
        match action {
            Action::OpenBrowser => self.open_in_browser(),
            _ => Vec::new(),
        }
    }

    /// The item's page, else the board's.
    pub(crate) fn open_in_browser(&self) -> Vec<Command> {
        self.action_item()
            .and_then(|i| i.url())
            .map(String::from)
            .or_else(|| self.project().map(|p| p.url.clone()))
            .map(|u| vec![Command::OpenUrl(u)])
            .unwrap_or_default()
    }

    /// The edit keys the item allows, for the status line, single-key edits first as in
    /// `a assignees · t labels · e field · X close`, then `< > move · J K reorder · space
    /// actions`. `None` with no item or nothing allowed.
    pub fn edit_hints(&self) -> Option<String> {
        let item = self.action_item()?;
        let ctx = self.edit_context();
        if crate::model::capabilities(item, &ctx).is_empty() {
            return None;
        }
        let key = |a: Action| self.keymap.keys_for(a).first().map(|k| k.label());
        let mut parts = Vec::new();
        for &action in HINTED {
            if let (Some(kind), Some(k)) = (self.allowed_kind(action, item), key(action)) {
                parts.push(format!("{k} {}", hint_word(action, kind)));
            }
        }
        if self.check_here(OpKind::Move, item).is_ok()
            && let (Some(p), Some(n)) = (key(Action::MovePrevColumn), key(Action::MoveNextColumn))
        {
            parts.push(format!("{p} {n} move"));
        }
        if self.check_here(OpKind::Reorder, item).is_ok()
            && let (Some(d), Some(u)) = (key(Action::ReorderDown), key(Action::ReorderUp))
        {
            parts.push(format!("{d} {u} reorder"));
        }
        if let Some(k) = key(Action::ActionMenu) {
            parts.push(format!("{k} actions"));
        }
        Some(parts.join(" · "))
    }
```

and add:

```rust
/// The bottom hint line: `left` cut with `…` to fit, then `? help`, which always shows
/// (pre-flight ruling 4).
pub fn status_line(left: &str, width: usize) -> String {
    const HELP: &str = "? help";
    if left.is_empty() {
        return truncate_to_width(HELP, width);
    }
    let tail = format!(" · {HELP}");
    let room = width.saturating_sub(display_width(&tail));
    format!("{}{tail}", truncate_to_width(left, room))
}
```

`src/ui/app/edit.rs`:

```rust
/// How long ago, roughly: `5s ago`, `2m ago`, `2h ago`.
pub fn ago(elapsed: std::time::Duration) -> String {
    let s = elapsed.as_secs();
    if s < 60 {
        format!("{s}s ago")
    } else if s < 3600 {
        format!("{}m ago", s / 60)
    } else {
        format!("{}h ago", s / 3600)
    }
}
```

In `impl App`:

```rust
    pub(crate) fn open_error_log(&mut self) -> Vec<Command> {
        self.error_sel = 0;
        self.mode = Mode::ErrorLog;
        Vec::new()
    }

    /// The error log lists failures newest first; `error_sel` counts from the newest.
    pub(crate) fn handle_error_log_key(&mut self, action: Option<Action>) -> Vec<Command> {
        let n = self.errors.len();
        match action {
            Some(Action::Down) => self.error_sel = (self.error_sel + 1).min(n.saturating_sub(1)),
            Some(Action::Up) => self.error_sel = self.error_sel.saturating_sub(1),
            Some(Action::Retry) => return self.retry_selected(),
            Some(Action::Back) | Some(Action::ErrorLog) => self.mode = self.base_mode(),
            Some(Action::Quit) => return self.request_quit(),
            _ => {}
        }
        Vec::new()
    }

    /// Sends the selected failed edit again. It leaves the log; when the matrix no longer
    /// allows it, it stays and the reason shows.
    fn retry_selected(&mut self) -> Vec<Command> {
        let Some(index) = self.errors.len().checked_sub(1 + self.error_sel) else {
            return Vec::new();
        };
        let failed = self.errors.remove(index);
        let cmds = self.submit(failed.kind, failed.op.clone());
        if cmds.is_empty() {
            self.errors.insert(index, failed);
        }
        self.error_sel = self.error_sel.min(self.errors.len().saturating_sub(1));
        cmds
    }
```

`src/ui/chrome.rs`:

```rust
fn draw_error_log(frame: &mut Frame, area: Rect, app: &App) {
    let rect = crate::ui::popup::centered(area, area.width.saturating_sub(4).min(100), area.height);
    let inner = rect.width.saturating_sub(2) as usize;
    let now = std::time::Instant::now();
    let mut lines: Vec<Line> = Vec::new();
    if app.errors.is_empty() {
        lines.push(Line::styled("No failed edits", app.theme.dim()));
    }
    for (row, failed) in app.errors.iter().rev().enumerate() {
        let text = format!(
            "{:>7}  Could not {}: {}",
            crate::ui::app::edit_ago(now.saturating_duration_since(failed.at)),
            failed.what,
            failed.message
        );
        let style = if row == app.error_sel { app.theme.selected() } else { ratatui::style::Style::default() };
        lines.push(Line::styled(pad_to_width(&truncate_to_width(&text, inner), inner), style));
    }
    let retry = app.keymap.keys_for(Action::Retry).first().map(|k| k.label()).unwrap_or_default();
    frame.render_widget(Clear, rect);
    frame.render_widget(
        Paragraph::new(lines).block(
            Block::default()
                .borders(Borders::ALL)
                .title(format!(" failed edits · {retry} retry · esc close ")),
        ),
        rect,
    );
}
```

```rust
    #[test]
    fn scopes_say_what_the_token_can_edit() {

        assert_eq!(s(&["public_repo", "project"]).level, Level::Ok);
        let boards_only = s(&["project"]);
        assert_eq!(boards_only.level, Level::Warn);
        assert!(boards_only.detail.contains("gh auth refresh -s repo"), "{}", boards_only.detail);
        let read = s(&["repo", "read:project"]);
        assert_eq!(read.level, Level::Warn);
        assert!(read.detail.contains("gh auth refresh -s project"));
        assert_eq!(s(&["repo"]).level, Level::Fail);
    }
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Implement**

Replace `scope_check`:

```rust
/// What the token can do. Editing boards needs `project`; editing issues and pull requests
/// also needs `repo` (or `public_repo` for public repositories). `None` means a fine-grained
/// token, which reports no scopes and cannot read boards owned by a user account at all.
pub fn scope_check(scopes: &Option<Vec<String>>) -> Check {
    let check = |level, detail: String| Check { level, name: "scopes", detail };
    let Some(s) = scopes else {
        return check(
            Level::Warn,
            "fine-grained token: works for organisation boards only; boards owned by a user account need `gh auth login`".into(),
        );
    };
    let has = |x: &str| s.iter().any(|y| y == x);
    let list = s.join(", ");
    match (has("project"), has("repo") || has("public_repo"), has("read:project")) {
        (true, true, _) => check(Level::Ok, format!("{list}; can edit boards and issues")),
        (true, false, _) => check(
            Level::Warn,
            format!("{list}; can edit boards; editing issues needs `gh auth refresh -s repo` (or public_repo)"),
        ),
        (false, _, true) => check(
            Level::Warn,
            format!("{list}; read:project only; editing needs `gh auth refresh -s project`"),
        ),
        (false, _, false) => check(
            Level::Fail,
            "missing the project scope; run `gh auth refresh -s project`".into(),
        ),
    }
}
```

```rust
    /// Adds a draft and returns its item.
    pub async fn add_draft_issue(&self, project: &ProjectId, title: &str, body: &str) -> Result<Item, GithubError> {
        let request = GraphqlRequest::from_body(queries::AddDraftIssue::build_query(
            queries::add_draft_issue::Variables {
                project: project.0.clone(),
                title: title.to_string(),
                body: body.to_string(),
            },
        ));
        let response = self.run(request).await?;
        item_payload(&response, "addProjectV2DraftIssue", "projectItem")
    }

    /// Removes the item from the project. A draft is deleted with it; an issue or pull
    /// request stays in its repository.
    pub async fn delete_item(&self, project: &ProjectId, item: &ItemId) -> Result<(), GithubError> {
        let request = GraphqlRequest::from_body(queries::DeleteItem::build_query(
            queries::delete_item::Variables { project: project.0.clone(), item: item.0.clone() },
        ));
        let response = self.run(request).await?;
        payload(&response, "deleteProjectV2Item").map(|_| ())
    }
```

```rust
//! Helpers shared by the live suites (tests/live.rs, tests/live_edits.rs): where the testbed
//! is, and a GitHub client that records with PB_RECORD=1.
#![allow(dead_code)] // each test crate uses a different part

use project_boards::github::Github;
use project_boards::github::record::RecordingTransport;
use project_boards::github::token::resolve_token_from_system;
use project_boards::github::transport::{HttpTransport, Transport};
use project_boards::model::*;
use std::sync::Arc;

fn testbed_toml() -> toml::Table {
    std::fs::read_to_string("tests/testbed.toml").unwrap().parse().unwrap()
}

pub fn testbed() -> (BoardRef, ProjectId) {
    let t = testbed_toml();
    let board = BoardRef {
        owner: t["owner"].as_str().unwrap().into(),
        number: t["number"].as_integer().unwrap() as u32,
    };
    (board, ProjectId::new(t["project_id"].as_str().unwrap()))
}

pub fn testbed_repo() -> RepoSlug {
    testbed_toml()["repo"].as_str().unwrap().parse().unwrap()
}

pub fn http() -> Arc<dyn Transport> {
    let token = resolve_token_from_system(None).expect("a token for the testbed");
    Arc::new(HttpTransport::new(token.value))
}

pub fn recording() -> bool {
    std::env::var("PB_RECORD").as_deref() == Ok("1")
}

/// A client for one test; with PB_RECORD=1 it records into tests/fixtures/recorded/<name>/.
pub fn github(name: &str) -> Github {
    let http = http();
    if recording() {
        let dir = std::path::Path::new("tests/fixtures/recorded").join(name);
        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&dir);
        Github::new(Arc::new(RecordingTransport::new(http, dir)))
    } else {
        Github::new(http)
    }
}

/// Prints the rate-limit points a test used, so the cost of writes stays visible.
pub fn report_points(name: &str, before: Option<u32>, after: Option<u32>) {
    match (before, after) {
        (Some(b), Some(a)) => println!("{name}: {} rate-limit points", b.saturating_sub(a)),
        _ => println!("{name}: rate-limit points unknown"),
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3b: Write `tests/live_edits.rs`**

```rust
//! Live writes against the testbed in tests/testbed.toml, and nothing else. Each test makes
//! its own throwaway draft, edits it, checks the result with a direct node lookup (which the
//! item index lag does not affect), and deletes the draft even when an assertion fails.
//!   cargo test --features live --test live_edits -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture
//!   PB_RECORD=1 cargo test --features live --test live_edits -- --test-threads=1
//! Each test prints the rate-limit points it used.
#![cfg(feature = "live")]

mod common;

use common::*;
use futures::FutureExt;
use project_boards::github::{Github, GithubError};
use project_boards::model::*;
use std::future::Future;
use std::panic::AssertUnwindSafe;
use std::sync::Arc;

/// Runs `body` on a fresh throwaway draft, then deletes the draft whatever happened.
async fn with_draft<F, Fut>(name: &str, body: F)
where
    F: FnOnce(Arc<Github>, Project, Item) -> Fut,
    Fut: Future<Output = ()>,
{
    let gh = Arc::new(github(name));
    let project = gh.resolve_project(&testbed().0).await.expect("the testbed resolves");
    let before = gh.rate().remaining;
    let draft = gh
        .add_draft_issue(&project.id, &format!("pb live: {name}"), "Throwaway item made by the live suite, which deletes it.")
        .await
        .expect("the throwaway draft is created");
    let outcome = AssertUnwindSafe(body(gh.clone(), project.clone(), draft.clone())).catch_unwind().await;
    // A separate client, so cleanup is never recorded and runs even if `gh` is in a bad state.
    if let Err(e) = Github::new(http()).delete_item(&project.id, &draft.id).await {
        eprintln!("CLEANUP FAILED: delete the draft '{}' ({}) from the testbed by hand: {e}", draft.title(), draft.id);
    }
    report_points(name, before, gh.rate().remaining);
    if let Err(panic) = outcome {
        std::panic::resume_unwind(panic);
    }
}

fn field<'a>(p: &'a Project, name: &str) -> &'a Field {
    p.fields.iter().find(|f| f.name == name).unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("testbed field {name}"))
}

async fn reload(gh: &Github, item: &ItemId) -> Item {
    gh.hydrate_items(std::slice::from_ref(item)).await.unwrap().remove(0)
}

/// Values compare by what they show; multi-selects by their option ids, in any order.
fn same(a: Option<&FieldValue>, b: &FieldValue) -> bool {
    match (a, b) {
        (Some(FieldValue::MultiSelect { option_ids: x, .. }), FieldValue::MultiSelect { option_ids: y, .. }) => {
            let (mut x, mut y) = (x.clone(), y.clone());
            x.sort();
            y.sort();
            x == y
        }
        (Some(a), b) => a.display() == b.display(),
        (None, _) => false,
    }
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn set_and_clear_every_field_type() {
    with_draft("edits_fields", |gh, p, draft| async move {
        let options = |name: &str| match &field(&p, name).kind {
            FieldKind::SingleSelect { options } | FieldKind::MultiSelect { options } => options.clone(),
            other => panic!("{name} is {other:?}"),
        };
        let sprint = match &field(&p, "Sprint").kind {
            FieldKind::Iteration { iterations, .. } => iterations[0].clone(),
            other => panic!("Sprint is {other:?}"),
        };
        let p0 = options("Priority")[0].clone();
        let areas = options("Areas");
        let cases = vec![
            ("Notes", FieldValue::Text("live note".into())),
            ("Size", FieldValue::Number(5.0)),
            ("Due", FieldValue::Date("2026-12-24".into())),
            ("Priority", FieldValue::SingleSelect { option_id: p0.id.clone(), name: p0.name.clone() }),
            (
                "Areas",
                FieldValue::MultiSelect {
                    option_ids: vec![areas[0].id.clone(), areas[1].id.clone()],
                    names: vec![areas[0].name.clone(), areas[1].name.clone()],
                },
            ),
            (
                "Sprint",
                FieldValue::Iteration {
                    iteration_id: sprint.id.clone(),
                    title: sprint.title.clone(),
                    start_date: sprint.start_date.clone(),
                },
            ),
        ];
        for (name, value) in &cases {
            let f = field(&p, name);
            let returned = gh.set_field_value(&p.id, &draft.id, &f.id, value).await.unwrap();
            assert!(same(returned.value(&f.id), value), "{name}: returned {:?}", returned.value(&f.id));
            let reloaded = reload(&gh, &draft.id).await;
            assert!(same(reloaded.value(&f.id), value), "{name}: reloaded {:?}", reloaded.value(&f.id));
        }
        for (name, _) in &cases {
            let f = field(&p, name);
            let returned = gh.clear_field_value(&p.id, &draft.id, &f.id).await.unwrap();
            assert!(returned.value(&f.id).is_none(), "{name} cleared");
        }
        let reloaded = reload(&gh, &draft.id).await;
        for (name, _) in &cases {
            assert!(reloaded.value(&field(&p, name).id).is_none(), "{name} stays cleared");
        }
    })
    .await;
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn reorder_to_the_top_and_after_another_item() {
    with_draft("edits_position", |gh, p, draft| async move {
        // The payload lists the project's order after the move (pre-flight ruling 14). If
        // GitHub ever lists something else there, report it rather than weaken the checks.
        let order = gh.update_item_position(&p.id, &draft.id, None).await.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(order.first(), Some(&draft.id), "first after moving to the top: {order:?}");
        let other = order.iter().find(|i| **i != draft.id).expect("a seeded item").clone();
        let order = gh.update_item_position(&p.id, &draft.id, Some(&other)).await.unwrap();
        let at = |id: &ItemId| order.iter().position(|i| i == id).expect("listed");
        assert_eq!(at(&draft.id), at(&other) + 1, "right after {other}: {order:?}");
    })
    .await;
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_refused_edit_carries_githubs_message() {
    with_draft("edits_refused", |gh, p, draft| async move {
        let f = field(&p, "Priority");
        let bogus = FieldValue::SingleSelect { option_id: OptionId::new("not-an-option"), name: "x".into() };
        let err = gh.set_field_value(&p.id, &draft.id, &f.id, &bogus).await.unwrap_err();
        println!("GitHub said: {err}");
        assert!(matches!(err, GithubError::Graphql(_)), "{err:?}");
    })
    .await;
}
```

```rust
#[tokio::test]
async fn recorded_field_edits_decode() {
    let (_, id) = testbed();

    let t = Arc::new(FixtureTransport::new());

        .set_field_value(&id, &draft.id, &FieldId::new("x"), &FieldValue::Text("live note".into()))
        .await
        .unwrap();

    let cleared = gh.clear_field_value(&id, &draft.id, &FieldId::new("x")).await.unwrap();
    assert!(!cleared.values.values().any(|v| v == &FieldValue::Text("live note".into())));
}
```

```
## Editing (0.2)

- [ ] On "Board", `>` moves a card to the next column at once; the header shows `saving 1…` briefly; github.com shows the move.
- [ ] `>` twice quickly from Todo moves the card to Done and GitHub ends there; a third `>` says "Already in the last column" and never clears Status; `<` on Todo says "Already in the first column".
- [ ] `K`/`J` reorder within a lane; on a sorted view they explain why not.
- [ ] `e` on an item edits Status, Priority, Notes (text), Size (number), Due (`fri`, then `+3d`, then empty to clear), Sprint (current one first) and Areas (Space toggles, Enter applies).
- [ ] "Bugs" view: move a bug to Done with `e`; it stays, dimmed with ⊘; `Tab` and back makes it go.
- [ ] A board view filtered with `f` and `-status:Done`: `>` from In Progress stops (Done is hidden); setting Status to Done with `e` shows a "Done (filtered)" column.
- [ ] `Space` lists the allowed operations with their keys; `Enter` and the listed keys run them.
- [ ] With the network off (Wi-Fi off), an edit is undone with an error; `E` lists it; back online, `R` retries it.
- [ ] `q` right after an edit asks "1 edit is still sending; quit anyway?".
- [ ] A board you can only read shows `read-only`; `>` says "This board is read-only for you".
- [ ] `doctor` says "can edit boards and issues".
```

### Task 23: Content ids, linked repositories and cache format 3

**Files:**
- Create: `src/model/refs.rs`
- Modify: `Cargo.toml`, `Cargo.lock`, `herdr-plugin.toml`, `src/model/mod.rs`, `src/model/item.rs`, `src/model/project.rs`, `src/model/caps.rs`, `src/github/convert.rs`, `crates/queries/graphql/board.graphql`, `src/store/cache.rs`, `src/ui/fixtures.rs`, `src/store/snapshot.rs` (test helper), `src/github/convert.rs` tests

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ContentFields`, `Item`, `Project`, `check` (0.1, Task 3).
- Produces:
  - `model::refs::{UserRef { id, login }, LabelRef { id, name, color }, MilestoneRef { id, title }, RepoRef { id, slug: RepoSlug }, CloseReason::{Completed, NotPlanned}, ContentId::{Issue(String), PullRequest(String), Draft(String)}}` (re-exported from `model`), with `ContentId::node_id(&self) -> &str`.
  - `ContentFields` gains `content_id: Option<String>` (the Issue, PullRequest or DraftIssue node id), `assignee_ids: BTreeMap<String, String>` (login → id), `label_ids: BTreeMap<String, String>` (name → id), `milestone: Option<MilestoneRef>`; the struct is `#[serde(default)]`.
  - `Item::content_id(&self) -> Option<ContentId>`; `Project.linked_repos: Vec<RepoRef>` (`#[serde(default)]`).

```rust
use crate::model::ids::RepoSlug;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct UserRef {
    pub id: String,
    pub login: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct LabelRef {
    pub id: String,
    pub name: String,
    /// Hex colour without `#`.
    pub color: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct MilestoneRef {
    pub id: String,
    pub title: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct RepoRef {
    pub id: String,
    pub slug: RepoSlug,
}

/// Why an issue is closed. Pull requests close without one.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum CloseReason {
    Completed,
    NotPlanned,
}

/// The node an issue, pull request or draft edit goes to.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum ContentId {
    Issue(String),
    PullRequest(String),
    Draft(String),
}

impl ContentId {
    pub fn node_id(&self) -> &str {
        match self {
            ContentId::Issue(id) | ContentId::PullRequest(id) | ContentId::Draft(id) => id,
        }
    }
}
```

```rust
    /// Where content edits go: the issue, pull request or draft behind the item.
    pub fn content_id(&self) -> Option<crate::model::ContentId> {
        use crate::model::ContentId;
        let id = self.content_fields.content_id.clone()?;
        match &self.content {
            ItemContent::Issue { .. } => Some(ContentId::Issue(id)),
            ItemContent::PullRequest { .. } => Some(ContentId::PullRequest(id)),
            ItemContent::Draft { .. } => Some(ContentId::Draft(id)),
            ItemContent::Redacted | ItemContent::Unknown { .. } => None,
        }
    }
```

```rust
/// Content edits go to the issue, pull request or draft node.
fn needs_content_id(item: &Item) -> Result<(), Denial> {
    if item.content_fields.content_id.is_some() {
        Ok(())
    } else {
        Err(Denial::NeedsRefresh)
    }
}
```

```rust
        assert_eq!(names, ["UpdateIssueTitle", "UpdatePullRequestBody", "UpdateDraftTitle", "SetIssueMilestone", "SetPullRequestMilestone"]);
        assert!(r[3].variables["milestone"].is_null(), "null clears the milestone");

        gh.close(&issue, None).await.unwrap();

        assert_eq!(r[4].operation_name, "ClosePullRequest");
        assert!(gh.close(&ContentId::Draft("DI_1".into()), None).await.is_err());
    }
```

- [ ] **Step 2: The operations (append to `board.graphql`)**

```graphql
# --- Mutations: issues, pull requests and drafts (0.3) -------------------------------------
# Title, body and milestone each have their own operation, so setting one never sends null
# for another (a null milestoneId clears the milestone).

mutation AddAssignees($id: ID!, $users: [ID!]!) {
  addAssigneesToAssignable(input: {assignableId: $id, assigneeIds: $users}) { clientMutationId }
}

mutation RemoveAssignees($id: ID!, $users: [ID!]!) {
  removeAssigneesFromAssignable(input: {assignableId: $id, assigneeIds: $users}) { clientMutationId }
}

mutation AddLabels($id: ID!, $labels: [ID!]!) {
  addLabelsToLabelable(input: {labelableId: $id, labelIds: $labels}) { clientMutationId }
}

mutation RemoveLabels($id: ID!, $labels: [ID!]!) {
  removeLabelsFromLabelable(input: {labelableId: $id, labelIds: $labels}) { clientMutationId }
}

mutation UpdateIssueTitle($id: ID!, $title: String!) {
  updateIssue(input: {id: $id, title: $title}) { issue { id } }
}

mutation UpdateIssueBody($id: ID!, $body: String!) {
  updateIssue(input: {id: $id, body: $body}) { issue { id } }
}

mutation SetIssueMilestone($id: ID!, $milestone: ID) {
  updateIssue(input: {id: $id, milestoneId: $milestone}) { issue { id } }
}

mutation UpdatePullRequestTitle($id: ID!, $title: String!) {
  updatePullRequest(input: {pullRequestId: $id, title: $title}) { pullRequest { id } }
}

mutation UpdatePullRequestBody($id: ID!, $body: String!) {
  updatePullRequest(input: {pullRequestId: $id, body: $body}) { pullRequest { id } }
}

mutation SetPullRequestMilestone($id: ID!, $milestone: ID) {
  updatePullRequest(input: {pullRequestId: $id, milestoneId: $milestone}) { pullRequest { id } }
}

mutation UpdateDraftTitle($id: ID!, $title: String!) {
  updateProjectV2DraftIssue(input: {draftIssueId: $id, title: $title}) { draftIssue { id } }
}

mutation UpdateDraftBody($id: ID!, $body: String!) {
  updateProjectV2DraftIssue(input: {draftIssueId: $id, body: $body}) { draftIssue { id } }
}

mutation SetDraftAssignees($id: ID!, $users: [ID!]!) {
  updateProjectV2DraftIssue(input: {draftIssueId: $id, assigneeIds: $users}) { draftIssue { id } }
}

mutation AddComment($subject: ID!, $body: String!) {
  addComment(input: {subjectId: $subject, body: $body}) { clientMutationId }
}

mutation CloseIssue($id: ID!, $reason: IssueClosedStateReason!) {
  closeIssue(input: {issueId: $id, stateReason: $reason}) { issue { id } }
}

mutation ReopenIssue($id: ID!) {
  reopenIssue(input: {issueId: $id}) { issue { id } }
}

mutation ClosePullRequest($id: ID!) {
  closePullRequest(input: {pullRequestId: $id}) { pullRequest { id } }
}

mutation ReopenPullRequest($id: ID!) {
  reopenPullRequest(input: {pullRequestId: $id}) { pullRequest { id } }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: The methods (in `src/github/mutations.rs`)**

```rust
use crate::model::{CloseReason, ContentId};

impl Github {
    /// Runs a mutation whose payload carries nothing the board needs.
    async fn mutate_ok<V: serde::Serialize>(
        &self,
        body: graphql_client::QueryBody<V>,
        name: &str,
    ) -> Result<(), GithubError> {
        let response = self.run(GraphqlRequest::from_body(body)).await?;
        payload(&response, name).map(|_| ())
    }

    pub async fn add_assignees(&self, content: &str, users: &[String]) -> Result<(), GithubError> {
        let vars = queries::add_assignees::Variables { id: content.into(), users: users.to_vec() };
        self.mutate_ok(queries::AddAssignees::build_query(vars), "addAssigneesToAssignable").await
    }

    pub async fn remove_assignees(&self, content: &str, users: &[String]) -> Result<(), GithubError> {
        let vars = queries::remove_assignees::Variables { id: content.into(), users: users.to_vec() };
        self.mutate_ok(queries::RemoveAssignees::build_query(vars), "removeAssigneesFromAssignable").await
    }

    /// Replaces a draft's assignees (drafts have no add/remove mutation).
    pub async fn set_draft_assignees(&self, draft: &str, users: &[String]) -> Result<(), GithubError> {
        let vars = queries::set_draft_assignees::Variables { id: draft.into(), users: users.to_vec() };
        self.mutate_ok(queries::SetDraftAssignees::build_query(vars), "updateProjectV2DraftIssue").await
    }

    pub async fn add_labels(&self, content: &str, labels: &[String]) -> Result<(), GithubError> {
        let vars = queries::add_labels::Variables { id: content.into(), labels: labels.to_vec() };
        self.mutate_ok(queries::AddLabels::build_query(vars), "addLabelsToLabelable").await
    }

    pub async fn remove_labels(&self, content: &str, labels: &[String]) -> Result<(), GithubError> {
        let vars = queries::remove_labels::Variables { id: content.into(), labels: labels.to_vec() };
        self.mutate_ok(queries::RemoveLabels::build_query(vars), "removeLabelsFromLabelable").await
    }

    pub async fn set_title(&self, content: &ContentId, title: &str) -> Result<(), GithubError> {
        let (id, title) = (content.node_id().to_string(), title.to_string());
        match content {
            ContentId::Issue(_) => {
                self.mutate_ok(queries::UpdateIssueTitle::build_query(queries::update_issue_title::Variables { id, title }), "updateIssue").await
            }
            ContentId::PullRequest(_) => {
                self.mutate_ok(queries::UpdatePullRequestTitle::build_query(queries::update_pull_request_title::Variables { id, title }), "updatePullRequest").await
            }
            ContentId::Draft(_) => {
                self.mutate_ok(queries::UpdateDraftTitle::build_query(queries::update_draft_title::Variables { id, title }), "updateProjectV2DraftIssue").await
            }
        }
    }

    pub async fn set_body(&self, content: &ContentId, body: &str) -> Result<(), GithubError> {
        let (id, body) = (content.node_id().to_string(), body.to_string());
        match content {
            ContentId::Issue(_) => {
                self.mutate_ok(queries::UpdateIssueBody::build_query(queries::update_issue_body::Variables { id, body }), "updateIssue").await
            }
            ContentId::PullRequest(_) => {
                self.mutate_ok(queries::UpdatePullRequestBody::build_query(queries::update_pull_request_body::Variables { id, body }), "updatePullRequest").await
            }
            ContentId::Draft(_) => {
                self.mutate_ok(queries::UpdateDraftBody::build_query(queries::update_draft_body::Variables { id, body }), "updateProjectV2DraftIssue").await
            }
        }
    }

    /// Sets (`Some(milestone id)`) or clears (`None`) the milestone. Drafts have none.
    pub async fn set_milestone(&self, content: &ContentId, milestone: Option<&str>) -> Result<(), GithubError> {
        let (id, milestone) = (content.node_id().to_string(), milestone.map(String::from));
        match content {
            ContentId::Issue(_) => {
                self.mutate_ok(queries::SetIssueMilestone::build_query(queries::set_issue_milestone::Variables { id, milestone }), "updateIssue").await
            }
            ContentId::PullRequest(_) => {
                self.mutate_ok(queries::SetPullRequestMilestone::build_query(queries::set_pull_request_milestone::Variables { id, milestone }), "updatePullRequest").await
            }
            ContentId::Draft(_) => Err(GithubError::Decode("drafts have no milestone".into())),
        }
    }

    pub async fn add_comment(&self, subject: &str, body: &str) -> Result<(), GithubError> {
        let vars = queries::add_comment::Variables { subject: subject.into(), body: body.into() };
        self.mutate_ok(queries::AddComment::build_query(vars), "addComment").await
    }

    /// Closes an issue (as completed unless `reason` says otherwise) or a pull request.
    pub async fn close(&self, content: &ContentId, reason: Option<CloseReason>) -> Result<(), GithubError> {
        use queries::close_issue::IssueClosedStateReason as Reason;
        let id = content.node_id().to_string();
        match content {
            ContentId::Issue(_) => {
                let reason = match reason {
                    Some(CloseReason::NotPlanned) => Reason::NOT_PLANNED,
                    Some(CloseReason::Completed) | None => Reason::COMPLETED,
                };
                self.mutate_ok(queries::CloseIssue::build_query(queries::close_issue::Variables { id, reason }), "closeIssue").await
            }
            ContentId::PullRequest(_) => {
                self.mutate_ok(queries::ClosePullRequest::build_query(queries::close_pull_request::Variables { id }), "closePullRequest").await
            }
            ContentId::Draft(_) => Err(GithubError::Decode("drafts cannot be closed".into())),
        }
    }

    pub async fn reopen(&self, content: &ContentId) -> Result<(), GithubError> {
        let id = content.node_id().to_string();
        match content {
            ContentId::Issue(_) => {
                self.mutate_ok(queries::ReopenIssue::build_query(queries::reopen_issue::Variables { id }), "reopenIssue").await
            }
            ContentId::PullRequest(_) => {
                self.mutate_ok(queries::ReopenPullRequest::build_query(queries::reopen_pull_request::Variables { id }), "reopenPullRequest").await
            }
            ContentId::Draft(_) => Err(GithubError::Decode("drafts cannot be reopened".into())),
        }
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: The operations (append to `board.graphql`) and their names in `operation!`**

```graphql
# --- Mutations: project items (0.3) --------------------------------------------------------

mutation AddItemById($project: ID!, $content: ID!) {
  addProjectV2ItemById(input: {projectId: $project, contentId: $content}) { item { ...ItemFields } }
}

mutation CreateIssue($repo: ID!, $title: String!, $body: String!) {
  createIssue(input: {repositoryId: $repo, title: $title, body: $body}) { issue { id } }
}

mutation ConvertDraft($item: ID!, $repo: ID!) {
  convertProjectV2DraftIssueItemToIssue(input: {itemId: $item, repositoryId: $repo}) { item { ...ItemFields } }
}

mutation ArchiveItem($project: ID!, $item: ID!) {
  archiveProjectV2Item(input: {projectId: $project, itemId: $item}) { item { id } }
}

mutation UnarchiveItem($project: ID!, $item: ID!) {
  unarchiveProjectV2Item(input: {projectId: $project, itemId: $item}) { item { id } }
}

# The live suite's cleanup only: the app never deletes issues.
mutation DeleteIssue($id: ID!) {
  deleteIssue(input: {issueId: $id}) { clientMutationId }
}
```

```rust
impl Github {
    /// Adds an existing issue or pull request (by node id) to the project and returns its
    /// item; GitHub returns the existing item when it is already there.
    pub async fn add_item_by_id(&self, project: &ProjectId, content: &str) -> Result<Item, GithubError> {
        let vars = queries::add_item_by_id::Variables { project: project.0.clone(), content: content.into() };
        let response = self.run(GraphqlRequest::from_body(queries::AddItemById::build_query(vars))).await?;
        item_payload(&response, "addProjectV2ItemById", "item")
    }

    /// Creates an issue and returns its node id; `add_item_by_id` puts it on the board.
    pub async fn create_issue(&self, repo: &str, title: &str, body: &str) -> Result<String, GithubError> {
        let vars = queries::create_issue::Variables { repo: repo.into(), title: title.into(), body: body.into() };
        let response = self.run(GraphqlRequest::from_body(queries::CreateIssue::build_query(vars))).await?;
        payload(&response, "createIssue")?["issue"]["id"]
            .as_str()
            .map(String::from)
            .ok_or_else(|| GithubError::Decode("createIssue returned no issue id".into()))
    }

    pub async fn convert_draft(&self, item: &ItemId, repo: &str) -> Result<Item, GithubError> {
        let vars = queries::convert_draft::Variables { item: item.0.clone(), repo: repo.into() };
        let response = self.run(GraphqlRequest::from_body(queries::ConvertDraft::build_query(vars))).await?;
        item_payload(&response, "convertProjectV2DraftIssueItemToIssue", "item")
    }

    pub async fn archive_item(&self, project: &ProjectId, item: &ItemId) -> Result<(), GithubError> {
        let vars = queries::archive_item::Variables { project: project.0.clone(), item: item.0.clone() };
        self.mutate_ok(queries::ArchiveItem::build_query(vars), "archiveProjectV2Item").await
    }

    pub async fn unarchive_item(&self, project: &ProjectId, item: &ItemId) -> Result<(), GithubError> {
        let vars = queries::unarchive_item::Variables { project: project.0.clone(), item: item.0.clone() };
        self.mutate_ok(queries::UnarchiveItem::build_query(vars), "unarchiveProjectV2Item").await
    }

    /// Deletes an issue. Only the live suite calls this, to clean up the issues it made.
    pub async fn delete_issue(&self, issue: &str) -> Result<(), GithubError> {
        let vars = queries::delete_issue::Variables { id: issue.into() };
        self.mutate_ok(queries::DeleteIssue::build_query(vars), "deleteIssue").await
    }
}
```

```rust
//! milestones; repositories the user can push to; issue and pull request search.

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[tokio::test]

            "assignableUsers": {"nodes": [{"id": "U_1", "login": "octo"}, null]},

        let slugs: Vec<String> = list.iter().map(|r| r.slug.to_string()).collect();

            {"__typename": "PullRequest", "id": "PR_1", "number": 13, "title": "Fix", "state": "MERGED", "repository": {"nameWithOwner": "acme/app"}},

        t.push("ContentByNumber", json!({"repository": {"issueOrPullRequest": null}}));
        let gh = Github::new(t.clone());
        let hits = gh.search_content("crash repo:acme/app").await.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(hits.len(), 2);
        assert_eq!((hits[0].pull, hits[0].open, hits[1].pull, hits[1].open), (false, true, true, false));
        assert_eq!(t.requests()[0].variables["query"], "crash repo:acme/app");
        let one = gh.content_by_number(&repo(), 12).await.unwrap().unwrap();
        assert_eq!((one.number, one.open), (12, false));
        assert_eq!(t.requests()[1].variables["number"], 12);
        assert_eq!(gh.content_by_number(&repo(), 99).await.unwrap(), None);
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: The model types (append to `src/model/refs.rs`, above the tests)**

```rust
/// What the assignee, label and milestone editors offer for one repository.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub struct RepoCandidates {
    pub repo: RepoRef,
    pub users: Vec<UserRef>,
    pub labels: Vec<LabelRef>,
    pub milestones: Vec<MilestoneRef>,
}

/// An issue or pull request found by search or by URL, to add to the board.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub struct ContentHit {
    /// The node id `addProjectV2ItemById` takes.
    pub id: String,
    pub pull: bool,
    /// `owner/name`
    pub repo: String,
    pub number: u32,
    pub title: String,
    pub open: bool,
}

/// `https://github.com/owner/name/issues/12`, `…/pull/12` (trailing path allowed) or
/// `owner/name#12`.
pub fn parse_content_url(text: &str) -> Option<(RepoSlug, u32)> {
    let t = text.trim();
    if let Some(rest) = t.strip_prefix("https://github.com/") {
        let parts: Vec<&str> = rest.split('/').collect();
        if parts.len() >= 4 && matches!(parts[2], "issues" | "pull") {
            let repo = format!("{}/{}", parts[0], parts[1]).parse().ok()?;
            return Some((repo, parts[3].parse().ok()?));
        }
        return None;
    }
    let (repo, number) = t.split_once('#')?;
    Some((repo.parse().ok()?, number.parse().ok()?))
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: The operations (append to `board.graphql`) and their names in `operation!`**

```graphql
# --- Lookups for the editors (0.3) ---------------------------------------------------------

query RepoCandidates($owner: String!, $name: String!) {
  repository(owner: $owner, name: $name) {
    id
    assignableUsers(first: 100) { nodes { id login } }
    labels(first: 100) { nodes { id name color } }
    milestones(first: 50, states: [OPEN], orderBy: {field: DUE_DATE, direction: ASC}) { nodes { id title } }
  }
}

query PushableRepos {
  viewer {
    repositories(first: 100, affiliations: [OWNER, COLLABORATOR, ORGANIZATION_MEMBER],
                 ownerAffiliations: [OWNER, COLLABORATOR, ORGANIZATION_MEMBER],
                 isArchived: false, orderBy: {field: PUSHED_AT, direction: DESC}) {
      nodes { id nameWithOwner viewerPermission hasIssuesEnabled }
    }
  }
}

query RepoLookup($owner: String!, $name: String!) {
  repository(owner: $owner, name: $name) { id nameWithOwner viewerPermission hasIssuesEnabled }
}

query SearchContent($query: String!) {
  search(query: $query, type: ISSUE, first: 20) {
    nodes {
      __typename
      ... on Issue { id number title state repository { nameWithOwner } }
      ... on PullRequest { id number title state repository { nameWithOwner } }
    }
  }
}

query ContentByNumber($owner: String!, $name: String!, $number: Int!) {
  repository(owner: $owner, name: $name) {
    issueOrPullRequest(number: $number) {
      __typename
      ... on Issue { id number title state repository { nameWithOwner } }
      ... on PullRequest { id number title state repository { nameWithOwner } }
    }
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: The methods (top of `src/github/lookups.rs`)**

```rust
use crate::github::convert::{nodes, str_of, typename};
use crate::github::queries;
use crate::github::transport::GraphqlRequest;
use crate::github::{Github, GithubError};
use crate::model::*;
use graphql_client::GraphQLQuery;
use serde_json::Value;

fn writable(repo: &Value) -> bool {
    matches!(repo["viewerPermission"].as_str(), Some("ADMIN" | "MAINTAIN" | "WRITE"))
        && repo["hasIssuesEnabled"].as_bool() == Some(true)
}

fn repo_ref(repo: &Value) -> Option<RepoRef> {
    Some(RepoRef {
        id: repo["id"].as_str()?.to_string(),
        slug: repo["nameWithOwner"].as_str()?.parse().ok()?,
    })
}

fn hit(node: &Value) -> Option<ContentHit> {
    let pull = match typename(node) {
        "Issue" => false,
        "PullRequest" => true,
        _ => return None,
    };
    Some(ContentHit {
        id: node["id"].as_str()?.to_string(),
        pull,
        repo: str_of(&node["repository"]["nameWithOwner"]),
        number: node["number"].as_u64()? as u32,
        title: str_of(&node["title"]),
        open: node["state"] == "OPEN",
    })
}

impl Github {
    /// The first 100 assignable users and labels and 50 open milestones of `repo`.
    pub async fn repo_candidates(&self, repo: &RepoSlug) -> Result<RepoCandidates, GithubError> {
        let vars = queries::repo_candidates::Variables { owner: repo.owner.clone(), name: repo.name.clone() };
        let data = self.run(GraphqlRequest::from_body(queries::RepoCandidates::build_query(vars))).await?.data;
        let r = &data["repository"];
        let id = r["id"]
            .as_str()
            .ok_or_else(|| GithubError::Decode(format!("repository {repo} not found or not visible")))?;
        Ok(RepoCandidates {
            repo: RepoRef { id: id.to_string(), slug: repo.clone() },
            users: nodes(r, "assignableUsers")
                .into_iter()
                .filter_map(|u| Some(UserRef { id: u["id"].as_str()?.into(), login: u["login"].as_str()?.into() }))
                .collect(),
            labels: nodes(r, "labels")
                .into_iter()
                .filter_map(|l| {
                    Some(LabelRef { id: l["id"].as_str()?.into(), name: str_of(&l["name"]), color: str_of(&l["color"]) })
                })
                .collect(),
            milestones: nodes(r, "milestones")
                .into_iter()
                .filter_map(|m| Some(MilestoneRef { id: m["id"].as_str()?.into(), title: str_of(&m["title"]) }))
                .collect(),
        })
    }

    /// The viewer's 100 most recently pushed repositories they can write to, issues on.
    pub async fn pushable_repos(&self) -> Result<Vec<RepoRef>, GithubError> {
        let body = queries::PushableRepos::build_query(queries::pushable_repos::Variables);
        let data = self.run(GraphqlRequest::from_body(body)).await?.data;
        Ok(nodes(&data["viewer"], "repositories")
            .into_iter()
            .filter(|r| writable(r))
            .filter_map(repo_ref)
            .collect())
    }

    /// `repo` when it exists, the viewer can write to it and it has issues; else `None`.
    pub async fn lookup_repo(&self, repo: &RepoSlug) -> Result<Option<RepoRef>, GithubError> {
        let vars = queries::repo_lookup::Variables { owner: repo.owner.clone(), name: repo.name.clone() };
        let data = self.run(GraphqlRequest::from_body(queries::RepoLookup::build_query(vars))).await?.data;
        let r = &data["repository"];
        Ok(if r.is_object() && writable(r) { repo_ref(r) } else { None })
    }

    /// Issues and pull requests matching GitHub search `query`, at most 20.
    pub async fn search_content(&self, query: &str) -> Result<Vec<ContentHit>, GithubError> {
        let vars = queries::search_content::Variables { query: query.into() };
        let data = self.run(GraphqlRequest::from_body(queries::SearchContent::build_query(vars))).await?.data;
        Ok(nodes(&data, "search").into_iter().filter_map(hit).collect())
    }

    /// Issue or pull request `number` of `repo`, if there is one.
    pub async fn content_by_number(&self, repo: &RepoSlug, number: u32) -> Result<Option<ContentHit>, GithubError> {
        let vars = queries::content_by_number::Variables {
            owner: repo.owner.clone(),
            name: repo.name.clone(),
            number: i64::from(number),
        };
        let data = self.run(GraphqlRequest::from_body(queries::ContentByNumber::build_query(vars))).await?.data;
        Ok(hit(&data["repository"]["issueOrPullRequest"]))
    }
}
```

```rust
            Op::Assignees { item, add, remove, .. } => {
                let key = value_key(snap, item, FieldKind::Assignees, |v| matches!(v, FieldValue::Users(_)));
                if let Some(i) = snap.items.get_mut(item) {
                    let mut logins: Vec<String> = i.assignees().iter().map(|s| s.to_string()).collect();
                    logins.retain(|l| !remove.iter().any(|u| &u.login == l));
                    for u in add {
                        if !logins.contains(&u.login) {
                            logins.push(u.login.clone());
                        }
                    }
                    for u in remove {
                        i.content_fields.assignee_ids.remove(&u.login);
                    }
                    for u in add {
                        i.content_fields.assignee_ids.insert(u.login.clone(), u.id.clone());
                    }
                    if let Some(key) = key {
                        i.values.insert(key, FieldValue::Users(logins));
                    }
                }
            }
            Op::Labels { item, add, remove, .. } => {
                let key = value_key(snap, item, FieldKind::Labels, |v| matches!(v, FieldValue::Labels(_)));
                if let Some(i) = snap.items.get_mut(item) {
                    let mut labels: Vec<Label> = i.labels().into_iter().cloned().collect();
                    labels.retain(|l| !remove.iter().any(|r| r.name == l.name));
                    for l in add {
                        if !labels.iter().any(|x| x.name == l.name) {
                            labels.push(Label { name: l.name.clone(), color: l.color.clone() });
                        }
                    }
                    for l in remove {
                        i.content_fields.label_ids.remove(&l.name);
                    }
                    for l in add {
                        i.content_fields.label_ids.insert(l.name.clone(), l.id.clone());
                    }
                    if let Some(key) = key {
                        i.values.insert(key, FieldValue::Labels(labels));
                    }
                }
            }
            Op::Milestone { item, milestone, .. } => {
                let key = value_key(snap, item, FieldKind::Milestone, |v| matches!(v, FieldValue::Milestone(_)));
                if let Some(i) = snap.items.get_mut(item) {
                    i.content_fields.milestone = milestone.clone();
                    if let Some(key) = key {
                        match milestone {
                            Some(m) => i.values.insert(key, FieldValue::Milestone(m.title.clone())),
                            None => i.values.remove(&key),
                        };
                    }
                }
            }
            Op::Title { item, title, .. } => {
                let key = value_key(snap, item, FieldKind::Title, |_| false);
                if let Some(i) = snap.items.get_mut(item) {
                    match &mut i.content {
                        ItemContent::Issue { title: t, .. }
                        | ItemContent::PullRequest { title: t, .. }
                        | ItemContent::Draft { title: t } => *t = title.clone(),
                        ItemContent::Redacted | ItemContent::Unknown { .. } => {}
                    }
                    if let Some(key) = key {
                        i.values.insert(key, FieldValue::Text(title.clone()));
                    }
                }
            }
            Op::Body { .. } | Op::Comment { .. } => {}
            Op::Close { item, reason, .. } => {
                if let Some(i) = snap.items.get_mut(item) {
                    set_state(&mut i.content, ContentState::Closed);
                    i.content_fields.state_reason = reason.map(|r| match r {
                        CloseReason::Completed => StateReason::Completed,
                        CloseReason::NotPlanned => StateReason::NotPlanned,
                    });
                }
            }
            Op::Reopen { item, .. } => {
                if let Some(i) = snap.items.get_mut(item) {
                    set_state(&mut i.content, ContentState::Open);
                    i.content_fields.state_reason = None;
                }
            }
```

with the helpers:

```rust
/// Where `item` keeps the value of a built-in field: the key it already uses for such a
/// value, else the project's field of `kind`.
fn value_key(
    snap: &BoardSnapshot,
    item: &ItemId,
    kind: FieldKind,
    is_value: impl Fn(&FieldValue) -> bool,
) -> Option<FieldId> {
    snap.items
        .get(item)
        .and_then(|i| i.values.iter().find(|(_, v)| is_value(v)).map(|(k, _)| k.clone()))
        .or_else(|| snap.project.fields.iter().find(|f| f.kind == kind).map(|f| f.id.clone()))
}

fn set_state(content: &mut ItemContent, to: ContentState) {
    if let ItemContent::Issue { state, .. } | ItemContent::PullRequest { state, .. } = content {
        *state = to;
    }
}
```

```rust
        Op::Assignees { content, add, remove, all, .. } => {
            match content {
                ContentId::Draft(id) => gh.set_draft_assignees(id, &ids(all, |u| &u.id)).await?,
                ContentId::Issue(id) | ContentId::PullRequest(id) => {
                    if !add.is_empty() {
                        gh.add_assignees(id, &ids(add, |u| &u.id)).await?;
                    }
                    if !remove.is_empty() {
                        gh.remove_assignees(id, &ids(remove, |u| &u.id)).await?;
                    }
                }
            }
            Ok(Confirmed::Applied)
        }
        Op::Labels { content, add, remove, .. } => {
            let id = content.node_id();
            if !add.is_empty() {
                gh.add_labels(id, &ids(add, |l| &l.id)).await?;
            }
            if !remove.is_empty() {
                gh.remove_labels(id, &ids(remove, |l| &l.id)).await?;
            }
            Ok(Confirmed::Applied)
        }
        Op::Milestone { content, milestone, .. } => gh
            .set_milestone(content, milestone.as_ref().map(|m| m.id.as_str()))
            .await
            .map(|()| Confirmed::Applied),
        Op::Title { content, title, .. } => gh.set_title(content, title).await.map(|()| Confirmed::Applied),
        Op::Body { content, body, .. } => gh.set_body(content, body).await.map(|()| Confirmed::Applied),
        Op::Comment { content, body, .. } => {
            gh.add_comment(content.node_id(), body).await.map(|()| Confirmed::Applied)
        }
        Op::Close { content, reason, .. } => gh.close(content, *reason).await.map(|()| Confirmed::Applied),
        Op::Reopen { content, .. } => gh.reopen(content).await.map(|()| Confirmed::Applied),
```

(`use crate::model::ContentId;`). When an add succeeds and the remove after it fails, the op fails and the board rolls back; the next poll shows the half that GitHub applied.

### Task 28: Ops for items: create, convert, archive, unarchive, remove

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/ops/mod.rs`, `src/ops/send.rs`, `src/store/mod.rs`, `src/model/item.rs`, `src/ui/app.rs` (the `OpDone` arm of `on_sync`), `src/ui/app/edit.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 25's `Github` methods; `RepoRef`, `ContentHit` (Tasks 23, 26); `MemoryStore` (Task 5).
- Produces:
  - `ops::NewItem::{Draft { title: String, body: String }, Issue { repo: RepoRef, title: String, body: String }, Existing { hit: ContentHit }}`.
  - `Op::{Convert { item: ItemId, repo: RepoRef }, Archive { item }, Unarchive { item }, Remove { item }, Create { temp: ItemId, new: NewItem, presets: Vec<(FieldId, FieldValue)> }}`; `Create` applies a placeholder item `temp` (`local:N`) with the presets, at the end of the project order.
  - `Confirmed::Created { temp: ItemId, item: Item, refused: Vec<(FieldId, GithubError)> }` (`refused`: every preset that could not be set on the created item; all presets are tried. Review fix round 1 replaced `warning: Option<GithubError>`; the App names each refused field, "Added 'T', but a field was not set: Status (reason)" or "but N fields were not set: …", and records a refused scope).
  - (Review fix round 1) `OpKind::Create` (label "add an item", not in `OpKind::ALL`; `check(Create, ..)` is the board check) is the kind `submit_new` records. `FailedEdit` gains `may_exist: bool`: a draft or new-issue `Create` that failed with `Network` or `Decode` is logged as `edit::MAYBE_CREATED` ("may have been created; check GitHub before retrying (<error>)"), as is one pending when the board reloads (`MAYBE_SENT_RELOADED`), and `R` refuses such an entry with `MAYBE_CREATED`. Opening the detail of a placeholder sends no `LoadDetail` (the detail says "Creating on GitHub…"); `renamed` retargets an open detail to the real item and `settle` returns `Command::LoadDetail` for it.
  - (Ruling E15, added in implementation) `Confirmed::NotAdded { issue: String, error: GithubError }`: `createIssue` succeeded but `addProjectV2ItemById` failed (any error, a rate limit included, so the queue never re-sends the Create). `Op::add_created(&self, issue: &str) -> Option<Op>` turns a new-issue `Create` into a `Create` of `NewItem::Existing` for that issue (number 0, shown without one); the App logs that op, so `R` only adds. A `Create` still pending when the board reloads is logged with `edit::MAYBE_SENT_RELOADED` instead of `NOT_SENT_RELOADED`.
  - The store's `settle_ok` returns `Some((temp, real))` for `Created` and keeps the created item through stale full loads for `RECENT_WINDOW`.
  - `Item::number()` is `None` for number 0 (placeholders show no `#0`).
  - `App::submit_new(&mut self, op: Op, needs_repo: bool) -> Vec<Command>` (selects and highlights the placeholder) and `App::renamed(&mut self, temp: &ItemId, real: &ItemId)`; `App::settle` returns `Vec<Command>` and reports a preset warning.

```rust
        let names: Vec<String> = t.requests().iter().map(|r| r.operation_name.clone()).collect();
        assert_eq!(names, ["CreateIssue", "AddItemById", "SetSingleSelectValue", "SetTextValue"]);
    }
```

with:

```rust
/// What a new item looks like until GitHub has created it.
fn placeholder(temp: &ItemId, new: &NewItem, presets: &[(FieldId, FieldValue)]) -> Item {
    let content = match new {
        NewItem::Draft { title, .. } => ItemContent::Draft { title: title.clone() },
        NewItem::Issue { repo, title, .. } => ItemContent::Issue {
            reference: ContentRef { repo: repo.slug.to_string(), number: 0, url: String::new() },
            title: title.clone(),
            state: ContentState::Open,
        },
        NewItem::Existing { hit } => {
            let reference = ContentRef { repo: hit.repo.clone(), number: hit.number, url: String::new() };
            let state = if hit.open { ContentState::Open } else { ContentState::Closed };
            if hit.pull {
                ItemContent::PullRequest { reference, title: hit.title.clone(), state, is_draft: false }
            } else {
                ItemContent::Issue { reference, title: hit.title.clone(), state }
            }
        }
    };
    Item {
        id: temp.clone(),
        content,
        archived: false,
        updated_at: String::new(),
        values: presets.iter().cloned().collect(),
        content_fields: ContentFields::default(),
    }
}
```

In `src/model/item.rs`, `number()` becomes `self.reference().map(|r| r.number).filter(|n| *n > 0)`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Sending them (`src/ops/send.rs`)**

```rust
use crate::ops::NewItem;

/// Creates the item, then sets each preset on it. A preset GitHub refuses is reported as a
/// warning: the item exists, and the user sees what is missing.
async fn create(gh: &Github, project: &ProjectId, temp: &ItemId, new: &NewItem, presets: &[(FieldId, FieldValue)]) -> Result<Confirmed, GithubError> {
    let mut item = match new {
        NewItem::Draft { title, body } => gh.add_draft_issue(project, title, body).await?,
        NewItem::Issue { repo, title, body } => {
            let issue = gh.create_issue(&repo.id, title, body).await?;
            gh.add_item_by_id(project, &issue).await?
        }
        NewItem::Existing { hit } => gh.add_item_by_id(project, &hit.id).await?,
    };
    let mut warning = None;
    for (field, value) in presets {
        match gh.set_field_value(project, &item.id, field, value).await {
            Ok(updated) => item = updated,
            Err(e) => {
                warning = Some(e);
                break;
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(Confirmed::Created { temp: temp.clone(), item, warning })
}
```

and arms:

```rust
        Op::Convert { item, repo } => gh.convert_draft(item, &repo.id).await.map(Confirmed::Item),
        Op::Archive { item } => gh.archive_item(project, item).await.map(|()| Confirmed::Applied),
        Op::Unarchive { item } => gh.unarchive_item(project, item).await.map(|()| Confirmed::Applied),
        Op::Remove { item } => gh.delete_item(project, item).await.map(|()| Confirmed::Applied),
        Op::Create { temp, new, presets } => create(gh, project, temp, new, presets).await,
```

`Recent` gains `Item(Item)`; `reapply_recent` handles it:

```rust
                Recent::Item(item) => {

```

`settle_ok` handles `Created` before the general path:

```rust
    fn settle_ok(&mut self, id: OpId, confirmed: Confirmed) -> Option<(ItemId, ItemId)> {
        let pos = self.pending.iter().position(|p| p.id == id)?;
        let Pending { op, .. } = self.pending.remove(pos);
        let now = (self.clock)();
        let renamed = match confirmed {
            Confirmed::Created { temp, item, .. } => {
                if let Some(c) = self.confirmed.as_mut() {
                    c.upsert_items(vec![item.clone()]);
                }
                let real = item.id.clone();
                self.recent.push((now, Recent::Item(item)));
                Some((temp, real))
            }
            other => {
                if let Some(c) = self.confirmed.as_mut() {
                    // A poll that removed the item meanwhile wins over the server's copy.
                    if let Confirmed::Item(item) = other
                        && c.items.contains_key(&item.id)
                    {
                        c.upsert_items(vec![item]);
                    }
                    op.apply(c);
                }
                self.recent.push((now, Recent::Op(op)));
                None
            }
        };
        self.rebuild();
        renamed
    }
```

- [ ] **Step 5: The App creates and renames (`src/ui/app/edit.rs`)**

```rust
use crate::model::check_new;

impl App {
    /// Adds a new item (`Op::Create`): the placeholder shows at once, selected and
    /// highlighted, and stays in this view until it is created.
    pub(crate) fn submit_new(&mut self, op: Op, needs_repo: bool) -> Vec<Command> {
        if let Err(denial) = check_new(needs_repo, &self.edit_context()) {
            self.status.flash = Some(self.denial_text(denial));
            return Vec::new();
        }
        let temp = op.item().clone();
        let id = self.store.begin(op);
        self.pending_kinds.insert(id, OpKind::SetField);
        self.stick(&temp);
        self.selected_id = Some(temp.clone());
        self.highlight = Some(temp);
        self.restore_selection();
        vec![Command::Send(id)]
    }

    /// A placeholder became a real item: follow it.
    pub(crate) fn renamed(&mut self, temp: &ItemId, real: &ItemId) {
        for s in &mut self.sticky {
            if &s.id == temp {
                s.id = real.clone();
            }
        }
        if self.selected_id.as_ref() == Some(temp) {
            self.selected_id = Some(real.clone());
        }
        if self.highlight.as_ref() == Some(temp) {
            self.highlight = Some(real.clone());
        }
    }
}
```

`settle` now returns `Vec<Command>` (the `on_sync` arm becomes `SyncEvent::OpDone { id, result } => cmds.extend(self.settle(id, result)),`) and its `Ok` arm is:

```rust

                if let Confirmed::Created { item, warning: Some(e), .. } = &confirmed {
                    self.status.error = Some(format!(
                        "Added '{}', but a field was not set: {}",
                        item.title(),
                        error_message(e)
                    ));
                }
                if let Some((temp, real)) = self.store.settle_ok(id, confirmed) {
                    self.renamed(&temp, &real);
                }
                Vec::new()
            }
```

```rust
impl SyncTask {
    /// The task's name for logs. Never the search text or a repository: the log carries no
    /// board content (Global Constraints).
    pub fn kind(&self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            SyncTask::Resolve => "Resolve",
            SyncTask::FullLoad => "FullLoad",
            SyncTask::Incremental => "Incremental",
            SyncTask::ViewIds(_) => "ViewIds",
            SyncTask::Hydrate => "Hydrate",
            SyncTask::Detail(_) => "Detail",
            SyncTask::Projects => "Projects",
            SyncTask::Candidates(_) => "Candidates",
            SyncTask::Repos => "Repos",
            SyncTask::RepoLookup(_) => "RepoLookup",
            SyncTask::Search(_) => "Search",
        }
    }
}
```

and to `store_update`'s `None` arm `| SyncEvent::Candidates { .. } | SyncEvent::Repos(_) | SyncEvent::RepoFound { .. } | SyncEvent::SearchResults { .. }`. Add:

```rust
/// The GitHub search for "add existing": open issues and pull requests. The user's words are
/// text: any word that is not plain is quoted (`"` and `\` escaped), so `org:x`, `-label:y`
/// or `OR` cannot change the search. `is:open`, `is:closed`, `is:issue` and `is:pr` pass
/// through (case-insensitive); `is:open` is added unless the user typed `is:open` or
/// `is:closed`. A valid `repo:owner/name` (plain names, owner at most 39) is kept and replaces
/// the default `scope`. The query is capped at 256 characters (`sync::search_plan` reports
/// dropped scope repositories; `App::start_search` flashes a note or fails early).
pub fn search_query(query: &str, scope: &[RepoSlug]) -> String {
    let mut parts = Vec::new();
    let mut repos = Vec::new();
    for word in query.split_whitespace() {
        if let Some(slug) = word.strip_prefix("repo:").and_then(|r| r.parse::<RepoSlug>().ok()) {
            repos.push(format!("repo:{slug}"));
        } else if plain_term(word) {
            parts.push(word.to_string());
        } else {
            parts.push(format!("\"{}\"", word.replace('\\', "\\\\").replace('"', "\\\"")));
        }
    }
    if repos.is_empty() {
        repos = scope.iter().map(|r| format!("repo:{r}")).collect();
    }
    parts.extend(repos);
    parts.push("is:open".into());
    parts.join(" ")
}
```

```rust
    pub async fn candidates(&self, repo: &RepoSlug) {
        match self.gh.repo_candidates(repo).await {
            Ok(candidates) => self.send(SyncEvent::Candidates { repo: repo.clone(), candidates }),
            Err(e) => self.fail(SyncTask::Candidates(repo.clone()), e),
        }
    }

    pub async fn repos(&self) {
        match self.gh.pushable_repos().await {
            Ok(list) => self.send(SyncEvent::Repos(list)),
            Err(e) => self.fail(SyncTask::Repos, e),
        }
    }

    pub async fn lookup_repo(&self, slug: &RepoSlug) {
        match self.gh.lookup_repo(slug).await {
            Ok(repo) => self.send(SyncEvent::RepoFound { slug: slug.clone(), repo }),
            Err(e) => self.fail(SyncTask::RepoLookup(slug.clone()), e),
        }
    }

    /// A pasted issue or pull request URL is looked up directly; anything else is searched.
    pub async fn search(&self, query: &str, scope: &[RepoSlug]) {
        let result = match parse_content_url(query) {
            Some((repo, number)) => self.gh.content_by_number(&repo, number).await.map(|h| h.into_iter().collect()),
            None => self.gh.search_content(&crate::sync::search_query(query, scope)).await,
        };
        match result {
            Ok(hits) => self.send(SyncEvent::SearchResults { query: query.to_string(), hits }),
            Err(e) => self.fail(SyncTask::Search(query.to_string()), e),
        }
    }
```

In `src/ui/app/edit.rs`:

```rust
use crate::model::{ContentHit, RepoCandidates, RepoRef, RepoSlug};

/// A lookup an editor waits on.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub enum Lookup<T> {
    Loading,
    Ready(T),
    Failed(String),
}

impl App {
    pub fn item(&self, id: &ItemId) -> Option<&Item> {
        self.snapshot()?.items.get(id)
    }

    /// Asks for `repo`'s candidates unless they are loaded or loading; after a failure, again.
    pub(crate) fn need_candidates(&mut self, repo: &RepoSlug) -> Vec<Command> {
        if matches!(self.candidates.get(repo), Some(Lookup::Ready(_) | Lookup::Loading)) {
            return Vec::new();
        }
        self.candidates.insert(repo.clone(), Lookup::Loading);
        vec![Command::LoadCandidates(repo.clone())]
    }

    pub(crate) fn need_repos(&mut self) -> Vec<Command> {
        if matches!(self.repos, Some(Lookup::Ready(_) | Lookup::Loading)) {
            return Vec::new();
        }
        self.repos = Some(Lookup::Loading);
        vec![Command::LoadRepos]
    }

    pub(crate) fn need_repo_lookup(&mut self, slug: &RepoSlug) -> Vec<Command> {
        if matches!(self.repo_lookups.get(slug), Some(Lookup::Ready(_) | Lookup::Loading)) {
            return Vec::new();
        }
        self.repo_lookups.insert(slug.clone(), Lookup::Loading);
        vec![Command::LookupRepo(slug.clone())]
    }

    pub(crate) fn start_search(&mut self, query: &str, scope: Vec<RepoSlug>) -> Vec<Command> {
        self.content_search = Some((query.to_string(), Lookup::Loading));
        vec![Command::Search { query: query.to_string(), scope }]
    }

    /// Where a draft's assignees come from: the pane's repository, else the project's first
    /// linked repository.
    pub fn draft_repo(&self) -> Option<RepoSlug> {
        self.pane_repo
            .clone()
            .or_else(|| self.project()?.linked_repos.first().map(|r| r.slug.clone()))
    }

    /// The repository whose users, labels and milestones `item` can take.
    pub fn candidate_repo(&self, item: &Item) -> Option<RepoSlug> {
        match item.reference() {
            Some(r) => r.repo.parse().ok(),
            None => self.draft_repo(),
        }
    }

    /// Records a failed lookup where its popover shows it. False for other failures.
    fn lookup_failed(&mut self, task: &SyncTask, error: &GithubError) -> bool {
        let message = format!("{}: {}", task_words(task), error_message(error));
        match task {
            SyncTask::Candidates(repo) => {
                self.candidates.insert(repo.clone(), Lookup::Failed(message));
            }
            SyncTask::Repos => self.repos = Some(Lookup::Failed(message)),
            SyncTask::RepoLookup(slug) => {
                self.repo_lookups.insert(slug.clone(), Lookup::Failed(message));
            }
            SyncTask::Search(query) => self.content_search = Some((query.clone(), Lookup::Failed(message))),
            _ => return false,
        }
        self.refresh_popup();
        true
    }

    /// Fills a popover that waits on a lookup which has landed or failed. Later tasks add
    /// the popovers that wait.
    pub(crate) fn refresh_popup(&mut self) {
        let Some(popup) = self.popup.take() else {
            return;
        };
        self.popup = Some(popup);
    }
}
```

`task_words` must be `pub(crate)` (or moved) so `edit.rs` can use it; export `Lookup` with `pub use edit::Lookup;`.

```rust
use crate::model::{ContentId, LabelRef, MilestoneRef, RepoCandidates, RepoSlug, UserRef};
use crate::ui::app::Lookup;

/// The users to offer: the repository's, plus current assignees it did not list (so they
/// can be removed), and the logins assigned now.
fn assignee_options(c: &RepoCandidates, item: &Item) -> (Vec<MultiOption<UserRef>>, Vec<String>) {
    let current: Vec<String> = item.assignees().iter().map(|s| s.to_string()).collect();
    let mut users = c.users.clone();
    for login in &current {
        if !users.iter().any(|u| &u.login == login)
            && let Some(id) = item.content_fields.assignee_ids.get(login)
        {
            users.push(UserRef { id: id.clone(), login: login.clone() });
        }
    }
    let options = users
        .into_iter()
        .map(|u| MultiOption { label: u.login.clone(), color: None, value: u })
        .collect();
    (options, current)
}

/// The labels to offer, current ones the repository did not list included.
fn label_options(c: &RepoCandidates, item: &Item) -> (Vec<MultiOption<LabelRef>>, Vec<String>) {
    let current: Vec<String> = item.label_names().iter().map(|s| s.to_string()).collect();
    let mut labels = c.labels.clone();
    for l in item.labels() {
        if !labels.iter().any(|x| x.name == l.name)
            && let Some(id) = item.content_fields.label_ids.get(&l.name)
        {
            labels.push(LabelRef { id: id.clone(), name: l.name.clone(), color: l.color.clone() });
        }
    }
    let options = labels
        .into_iter()
        .map(|l| MultiOption { label: l.name.clone(), color: Some(l.color.clone()), value: l })
        .collect();
    (options, current)
}

/// Open milestones (the item's own marked `✓`, listed even when closed), then "No milestone".
fn milestone_choices(c: &RepoCandidates, item: &Item) -> Vec<Choice<Option<MilestoneRef>>> {
    let current = item.content_fields.milestone.clone();
    let mut list = c.milestones.clone();
    if let Some(m) = &current
        && !list.contains(m)
    {
        list.push(m.clone());
    }
    let mut out: Vec<_> = list
        .into_iter()
        .map(|m| {
            let mark = if current.as_ref() == Some(&m) { "✓" } else { "" };
            Choice::new(m.title.clone(), Some(m)).with_detail(mark)
        })
        .collect();
    if current.is_some() {
        out.push(Choice::new("No milestone", None));
    }
    out
}

impl App {
    /// `a`, `t`, or the milestone entry: a popover over the item's repository candidates,
    /// loaded on first need.
    pub(crate) fn open_candidates(&mut self, kind: OpKind) -> Vec<Command> {
        let Some(item) = self.action_item().cloned() else {
            return Vec::new();
        };
        if let Err(denial) = check(kind, &item, &self.edit_context()) {
            self.status.flash = Some(self.denial_text(denial));
            return Vec::new();
        }
        let Some(repo) = self.candidate_repo(&item) else {
            self.status.flash = Some(
                "Open the board from a repository, or link one to the project, to assign drafts".into(),
            );
            return Vec::new();
        };
        let title = |what: &str| format!("{what} · {}", truncate_to_width(item.title(), 40));
        let (id, r) = (item.id.clone(), repo.clone());
        let popup = match kind {
            OpKind::Assignees => Popup::Assignees { item: id, repo: r, select: MultiSelect::loading(title("Assignees")) },
            OpKind::Labels => Popup::Labels { item: id, repo: r, select: MultiSelect::loading(title("Labels")) },
            _ => Popup::Milestone { item: id, repo: r, picker: ListPicker::loading(title("Milestone")) },
        };
        self.open_popup(popup);
        let cmds = self.need_candidates(&repo);
        self.refresh_popup();
        cmds
    }

    fn submit_people(&mut self, kind: OpKind, item: ItemId, op: impl FnOnce(ContentId) -> Op) -> Vec<Command> {
        match self.item(&item).and_then(|i| i.content_id()) {
            Some(content) => self.submit(kind, op(content)),
            None => {
                self.status.flash = Some(self.denial_text(crate::model::Denial::NeedsRefresh));
                Vec::new()
            }
        }
    }
}
```

Add one helper both checklists fill through (pre-flight ruling 16):

```rust
/// Fills a waiting assignee or label checklist once its repository's candidates have loaded
/// (or failed). `options` lists the choices and the names checked now; `name` names a choice.
pub(super) fn fill_checklist<T: Clone>(
    select: &mut MultiSelect<T>,
    lookup: Option<&Lookup<RepoCandidates>>,
    item: Option<&Item>,
    options: impl Fn(&RepoCandidates, &Item) -> (Vec<MultiOption<T>>, Vec<String>),
    name: impl Fn(&T) -> String,
) {
    match lookup {
        Some(Lookup::Ready(c)) => {
            if let Some(it) = item {
                let (choices, current) = options(c, it);
                select.set_options(choices, |v| current.contains(&name(v)));
            }
        }
        Some(Lookup::Failed(e)) => {
            select.loading = false;
            select.error = Some(e.clone());
        }
        Some(Lookup::Loading) | None => {}
    }
}
```

```rust
        let Some(mut popup) = self.popup.take() else {
            return;
        };
        match &mut popup {
            Popup::Assignees { item, repo, select } if select.loading => fill_checklist(
                select,
                self.candidates.get(repo),
                self.item(item),
                assignee_options,
                |u: &UserRef| u.login.clone(),
            ),
            Popup::Labels { item, repo, select } if select.loading => fill_checklist(
                select,
                self.candidates.get(repo),
                self.item(item),
                label_options,
                |l: &LabelRef| l.name.clone(),
            ),
            Popup::Milestone { item, repo, picker } if picker.loading => match self.candidates.get(repo) {
                Some(Lookup::Ready(c)) => {
                    if let Some(it) = self.item(item) {
                        picker.set_choices(milestone_choices(c, it));
                    }
                }
                Some(Lookup::Failed(e)) => {
                    picker.loading = false;
                    picker.error = Some(e.clone());
                }
                _ => {}
            },
            _ => {}
        }
        self.popup = Some(popup);
    }
```

(`fill_checklist`, `assignee_options`, `label_options` and `milestone_choices` are `pub(super)` in `popups.rs` so `edit.rs` can call them; or move `refresh_popup` into `popups.rs` — either is fine, keep it in one place.)

The two checklists also apply through one helper (in `impl App`, `popups.rs`):

```rust
    /// Enter or Esc in the assignee or label checklist: Esc closes; Enter sends only what
    /// changed (`op` builds the op from the item, its content id, added, removed and the whole
    /// new list). `None` while the checklist stays open.
    fn apply_checklist<T>(
        &mut self,
        outcome: MultiOutcome<T>,
        item: ItemId,
        kind: OpKind,
        op: impl FnOnce(ItemId, ContentId, Vec<T>, Vec<T>, Vec<T>) -> Op,
    ) -> Option<Vec<Command>> {
        match outcome {
            MultiOutcome::None => None,
            MultiOutcome::Cancel => {
                self.close_popup();
                Some(Vec::new())
            }
            MultiOutcome::Apply { added, removed, chosen } => {
                self.close_popup();
                if added.is_empty() && removed.is_empty() {
                    return Some(Vec::new());
                }
                let target = item.clone();
                Some(self.submit_people(kind, target, |content| op(item, content, added, removed, chosen)))
            }
        }
    }
```

New arms in `handle_popup_key`:

```rust
            Popup::Assignees { item, select, .. } => {
                let (item, outcome) = (item.clone(), select.handle(&key));
                let built = |item, content, add, remove, all| Op::Assignees { item, content, add, remove, all };
                if let Some(cmds) = self.apply_checklist(outcome, item, OpKind::Assignees, built) {
                    return cmds;
                }
            }
            Popup::Labels { item, select, .. } => {
                let (item, outcome) = (item.clone(), select.handle(&key));
                let built = |item, content, add, remove, _all| Op::Labels { item, content, add, remove };
                if let Some(cmds) = self.apply_checklist(outcome, item, OpKind::Labels, built) {

            Popup::Milestone { item, picker, .. } => match picker.handle(&key) {

                    let item = item.clone();
                    self.close_popup();
                    let current = self.item(&item).and_then(|i| i.content_fields.milestone.clone());
                    if current == milestone {
                        return Vec::new();

                    return self.submit_people(OpKind::Milestone, item.clone(), |content| Op::Milestone {
                        item,
                        content,
                        milestone,
                    });
                }
                ListOutcome::Cancel => {
                    self.close_popup();
                    return Vec::new();
                }
                ListOutcome::None | ListOutcome::Key(_) => {}
            },
```
