- [ ] **Step 2: The editor (top of `src/ui/editor.rs`)**

```rust
use std::path::Path;

/// `$VISUAL`, else `$EDITOR`, split on whitespace (so `code -w` works), else `vi`.
pub fn editor_command(env: impl Fn(&str) -> Option<String>) -> Vec<String> {
    for var in ["VISUAL", "EDITOR"] {
        if let Some(value) = env(var).filter(|v| !v.trim().is_empty()) {
            return value.split_whitespace().map(String::from).collect();
        }
    }
    vec!["vi".to_string()]
}

/// Writes `initial` to a private (0600) file in `dir`, runs `command` with the file as its
/// last argument, and returns what the file holds afterwards. `Ok(None)` when the editor
/// exits with an error. The file is removed either way.
pub fn edit_text(initial: &str, command: &[String], dir: &Path) -> std::io::Result<Option<String>> {
    let path = dir.join(format!("edit-{}.md", std::process::id()));
    crate::fsutil::write_atomic(&path, initial.as_bytes())?;
    let (program, args) = command
        .split_first()
        .ok_or_else(|| std::io::Error::other("no editor command"))?;
    let status = std::process::Command::new(program).args(args).arg(&path).status();
    let result = match status {
        Ok(s) if s.success() => std::fs::read_to_string(&path).map(Some),
        Ok(_) => Ok(None),
        Err(e) => Err(e),
    };
    let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&path);
    result
}

/// The first non-empty line is the title; everything after it is the body (trimmed).
/// `None` when there is no title.
pub fn title_and_body(text: &str) -> Option<(String, String)> {
    let mut lines = text.lines().skip_while(|l| l.trim().is_empty());
    let title = lines.next()?.trim().to_string();
    let body = lines.collect::<Vec<_>>().join("\n").trim().to_string();
    Some((title, body))
}
```

In `src/ui/keymap.rs`: `Action::Comment` (`comment`, `"comment on the item"`, key `c`, kinds `[OpKind::Comment]`); `Action::ALL` has 33 entries.

In `src/ui/app/edit.rs`: `EDIT_ACTIONS` gains `Action::Comment` after `Action::EditField`; `HINTED` becomes `[Assignees, Labels, EditField, Comment]`; `edit_key` gains `Action::Comment => self.comment(),`; and:

```rust
    /// The item `kind` would act on, when the matrix allows it; otherwise shows why.
    fn guarded(&mut self, kind: OpKind) -> Option<Item> {
        let item = self.action_item()?.clone();
        match self.check_here(kind, &item) {
            Ok(()) => Some(item),
            Err(reason) => {
                self.status.flash = Some(reason);
                None
            }
        }
    }

    pub(crate) fn edit_title(&mut self) -> Vec<Command> {
        let Some(item) = self.guarded(OpKind::Title) else { return Vec::new() };
        vec![Command::Edit { purpose: EditPurpose::Title(item.id.clone()), initial: item.title().to_string() }]
    }

    /// The body is only known once the item detail has loaded it: until then this opens the
    /// detail and asks to try again.
    pub(crate) fn edit_body(&mut self) -> Vec<Command> {
        let Some(item) = self.guarded(OpKind::Body) else { return Vec::new() };
        match self.loaded_body(&item.id) {
            Some(body) => vec![Command::Edit { purpose: EditPurpose::Body(item.id), initial: body }],
            None => {
                let cmds = if self.detail.as_ref().is_some_and(|d| d.item == item.id) {
                    Vec::new()
                } else {
                    self.open_detail(item.id.clone())
                };
                self.status.flash = Some("Loading the body; choose Edit body again once it shows".into());
                cmds
            }
        }
    }

    pub(crate) fn comment(&mut self) -> Vec<Command> {
        let Some(item) = self.guarded(OpKind::Comment) else { return Vec::new() };
        vec![Command::Edit { purpose: EditPurpose::Comment(item.id), initial: String::new() }]
    }

    fn loaded_body(&self, item: &ItemId) -> Option<String> {
        let d = self.detail.as_ref().filter(|d| &d.item == item)?;
        d.detail.as_ref().map(|x| x.body.clone())
    }

    /// The editor closed: send the edit unless it was aborted, empty or unchanged.
    pub(crate) fn on_edited(&mut self, purpose: EditPurpose, text: Option<String>) -> Vec<Command> {
        let Some(text) = text else {
            self.status.flash = Some("The editor exited with an error; nothing was sent".into());
            return Vec::new();
        };
        let say = |app: &mut App, message: &str| {
            app.status.flash = Some(message.to_string());
            Vec::new()
        };
        match purpose {
            EditPurpose::Title(id) => {
                let Some(item) = self.item(&id).cloned() else { return Vec::new() };
                let title = text.lines().map(str::trim).find(|l| !l.is_empty()).unwrap_or("").to_string();
                if title.is_empty() {
                    return say(self, "Empty title; nothing was sent");
                }
                if title == item.title() {
                    return say(self, "Title unchanged");
                }
                let Some(content) = item.content_id() else { return say(self, "This item can't be retitled") };
                self.submit(OpKind::Title, Op::Title { item: id, content, title })
            }
            EditPurpose::Body(id) => {
                let current = self.loaded_body(&id).unwrap_or_default();
                let body = text.trim_end().to_string();
                if body == current.trim_end() {
                    return say(self, "Body unchanged");
                }
                let Some(content) = self.item(&id).and_then(|i| i.content_id()) else { return Vec::new() };
                self.submit(OpKind::Body, Op::Body { item: id, content, body })
            }
            EditPurpose::Comment(id) => {
                let body = text.trim().to_string();
                if body.is_empty() {
                    return say(self, "Empty comment; nothing was sent");
                }
                let Some(content) = self.item(&id).and_then(|i| i.content_id()) else { return Vec::new() };
                self.submit(OpKind::Comment, Op::Comment { item: id, content, body })
            }
        }
    }
```

and:

```rust
/// Runs `$EDITOR` on `initial` with the TUI suspended, then restores it. `None` when the
/// editor could not run or exited with an error.
fn suspended_edit(terminal: &mut DefaultTerminal, initial: &str, dir: &std::path::Path) -> Option<String> {
    let _ = execute!(std::io::stdout(), DisableFocusChange);
    ratatui::restore();
    let command = crate::ui::editor::editor_command(|k| std::env::var(k).ok());
    let result = tokio::task::block_in_place(|| crate::ui::editor::edit_text(initial, &command, dir));
    match ratatui::try_init() {
        Ok(t) => *terminal = t,
        Err(e) => tracing::error!(error = %e, "could not restore the terminal after the editor"),
    }
    let _ = execute!(std::io::stdout(), EnableFocusChange);
    let _ = terminal.clear();
    // Anything crossterm's reader buffered while the editor ran (keys typed into the editor)
    // is dropped here, never replayed on the board (pre-flight ruling 10).
    while crossterm::event::poll(Duration::ZERO).unwrap_or(false) {
        let _ = crossterm::event::read();
    }
    match result {
        Ok(text) => text,
        Err(e) => {
            tracing::warn!(error = %e, "could not run the editor");
            None
        }
    }
}
```

Editor effects only ever come from key handling, which runs before the loop reads input again, so the old reader is never waiting on the terminal while the editor runs (Decision 27); and whatever input is still buffered when the editor returns is drained before the new reader starts. The smoke checklist (Task 38) checks that no keystroke typed in the editor reaches the board.

```rust
    /// `X`: reopen a closed item at once; close an open one after asking (a reason for an
    /// issue, yes or no for a pull request).
    pub(crate) fn close_or_reopen(&mut self) -> Vec<Command> {
        let Some(item) = self.action_item().cloned() else { return Vec::new() };
        let ctx = self.edit_context();
        let kind = match (check(OpKind::Close, &item, &ctx), check(OpKind::Reopen, &item, &ctx)) {
            (Ok(()), _) => OpKind::Close,
            (_, Ok(())) => OpKind::Reopen,
            (Err(close), Err(reopen)) => {
                // The reason that explains the item's state: reopen's for closed items.
                let shown = if matches!(close, crate::model::Denial::AlreadyClosed) { reopen } else { close };
                self.status.flash = Some(self.denial_text(shown));
                return Vec::new();
            }
        };
        let Some(content) = item.content_id() else { return Vec::new() };
        let reference = match item.number() {
            Some(n) => format!("#{n} '{}'", crate::ui::text::truncate_to_width(item.title(), 40)),
            None => format!("'{}'", crate::ui::text::truncate_to_width(item.title(), 40)),
        };
        match (kind, &content) {
            (OpKind::Reopen, _) => self.submit(kind, Op::Reopen { item: item.id, content }),
            (_, ContentId::Issue(_)) => {
                let picker = ListPicker::new(
                    format!("Close {reference}?"),
                    vec![
                        Choice::new("Close as completed", CloseReason::Completed),
                        Choice::new("Close as not planned", CloseReason::NotPlanned),
                    ],
                );
                self.open_popup(Popup::CloseReason { item: item.id, picker });
                Vec::new()
            }
            _ => {
                self.confirm = Some(Confirm {
                    question: format!("Close pull request {reference}? (y/n)"),
                    action: ConfirmAction::Submit { kind, op: Op::Close { item: item.id, content, reason: None } },
                });
                self.mode = Mode::Confirm;
                Vec::new()
            }
        }
    }
```

(imports: `ContentId`, `CloseReason`, `Choice`, `ListPicker`, `Popup`.)

`src/ui/app/popups.rs`: the variant `CloseReason { item: ItemId, picker: ListPicker<CloseReason> }` and its arm:

```rust
            Popup::CloseReason { item, picker } => match picker.handle(&key) {
                ListOutcome::Chosen(reason) => {
                    let item = item.clone();
                    self.close_popup();
                    // Choosing a reason is the confirmation (Decision 18).
                    return self.submit_people(OpKind::Close, item.clone(), |content| Op::Close {
                        item,
                        content,
                        reason: Some(reason),
                    });
                }
                ListOutcome::Cancel => {
                    self.close_popup();
                    return Vec::new();
                }
                ListOutcome::None | ListOutcome::Key(_) => {}
            },
```

`draw_popup`: `Popup::CloseReason { picker, .. } => render_list_picker(frame, area, picker, theme),`.

- [ ] **Step 2: Implement**

`src/ui/keymap.rs`: `Action::Archive` (`archive`, `"archive or unarchive the item"`, key `A`).

`src/ui/app/edit.rs`: `EDIT_ACTIONS` gains `Action::Archive` after `Action::CloseReopen`; the hints (Decision E18) list `a assignees · t labels · e field · < > move · X close · c comment · J K reorder · A archive`, so `HINTED` is `[Assignees, Labels, EditField]`, `HINTED_AFTER_MOVE` is `[CloseReopen, Comment]` and `HINTED_LAST` is `[Archive]` (`edit_hints` lists them around move and reorder); `edit_key` gains `Action::Archive => self.archive_or_unarchive(),`; and:

```rust
    /// `A`: archive the item, or unarchive an archived one. No confirmation: unarchiving
    /// undoes it.
    pub(crate) fn archive_or_unarchive(&mut self) -> Vec<Command> {
        let Some(item) = self.action_item().cloned() else { return Vec::new() };
        let (kind, op) = if item.archived {
            (OpKind::Unarchive, Op::Unarchive { item: item.id })
        } else {
            (OpKind::Archive, Op::Archive { item: item.id })
        };
        self.submit(kind, op)
    }

    /// Asks before removing the item from the project.
    pub(crate) fn confirm_remove(&mut self) -> Vec<Command> {
        let Some(item) = self.guarded(OpKind::Remove) else { return Vec::new() };
        let title = crate::ui::text::truncate_to_width(item.title(), 40);
        let question = match (&item.content, item.number()) {
            (ItemContent::Draft { .. }, _) => {
                format!("Remove the draft '{title}' from the project? The draft is deleted. (y/n)")
            }
            (_, Some(n)) => format!("Remove #{n} '{title}' from the project? It stays in its repository. (y/n)"),
            (_, None) => format!("Remove '{title}' from the project? (y/n)"),
        };
        self.confirm = Some(Confirm {
            question,
            action: ConfirmAction::Submit { kind: OpKind::Remove, op: Op::Remove { item: item.id } },
        });
        self.mode = Mode::Confirm;
        Vec::new()
    }

    /// "Show archived items" in the action menu: toggles `is:archived` in the session filter.
    pub(crate) fn toggle_archived(&mut self) -> Vec<Command> {
        let current = self.extra_filter().unwrap_or_default().to_string();
        let mut words: Vec<&str> = current.split_whitespace().collect();
        let had = words.iter().any(|w| w.eq_ignore_ascii_case("is:archived"));
        words.retain(|w| !w.eq_ignore_ascii_case("is:archived"));
        if !had {
            words.push("is:archived");
        }
        self.set_extra_filter(&words.join(" "))
    }
```

(`set_extra_filter` is private in `app.rs`; child modules can call it.)

`src/ui/app/popups.rs`: `MenuEntry::{Remove, ToggleArchived}`. `open_action_menu` no longer returns early without an item: build the item entries when there is one (as before, plus `Remove` — "Remove from project", after "Edit body", when allowed), then always add

```rust
        if self.current_view().is_some() {
            let shown = self.extra_filter().is_some_and(|f| f.split_whitespace().any(|w| w.eq_ignore_ascii_case("is:archived")));
            let label = if shown { "Hide archived items" } else { "Show archived items" };
            choices.push(Choice::new(label, MenuEntry::ToggleArchived));
        }
```

and show the item's denial only when there are no entries at all. `run_menu_entry` gains `MenuEntry::Remove => self.confirm_remove(),` and `MenuEntry::ToggleArchived => self.toggle_archived(),`. `menu_label` maps `Action::Archive` with `OpKind::Unarchive` to "Unarchive" and with `Archive` to "Archive" (the default capitalised label).

```rust
use crate::model::{RepoRef, check_new};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum NewKind {
    Issue,
    Draft,
}

/// What a chosen repository is for.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub enum RepoPurpose {
    NewIssue { presets: Vec<(FieldId, FieldValue)> },
}
```

the `Popup` variants of the Interfaces block, and in `impl App`:

```rust
    /// The values a new item starts with: on a board, the selected column's and the selected
    /// card's swimlane (spec section 6, "Where a new item lands"); none in a table.
    pub(crate) fn new_item_presets(&self) -> Vec<(FieldId, FieldValue)> {
        if self.layout().0 != Layout::Board {
            return Vec::new();
        }
        let (Some(field), Some(view), Some(project)) = (self.column_field(), self.current_view(), self.project())
        else {
            return Vec::new();
        };
        let mut out = Vec::new();
        if let Some(column) = self.board_columns().get(self.board_sel.column)
            && let Some(key) = &column.bucket.key
            && let Some(value) = field.value_for_bucket(key)
        {
            out.push((field.id.clone(), value));
        }
        if let (Some(lane), Some(item)) = (view.group_field(&project.fields), self.selected_item())
            && lane.kind.can_be_column()
            && let Some(value) = item.value(&lane.id)
        {
            out.push((lane.id.clone(), value.clone()));
        }
        out
    }

    /// `n`: what to add.
    pub(crate) fn open_new(&mut self) -> Vec<Command> {
        if let Err(denial) = check_new(false, &self.edit_context()) {
            self.status.flash = Some(self.denial_text(denial));
            return Vec::new();
        }
        let presets = self.new_item_presets();
        let picker = ListPicker::new(
            "New item",
            vec![Choice::new("New issue", NewKind::Issue), Choice::new("New draft", NewKind::Draft)],
        );
        self.open_popup(Popup::NewKind { presets, picker });
        Vec::new()
    }

    /// Repositories to pick from, in Decision 23's order and deterministic: the pane's
    /// repository, the project's linked ones, repositories found by typing `owner/name`
    /// (sorted by name), then those the user can push to (most recently pushed first). Each
    /// is listed once, at its first place. The flag says the list is still loading.
    pub(crate) fn repo_choices(&self) -> (Vec<Choice<RepoRef>>, bool) {
        let mut out: Vec<Choice<RepoRef>> = Vec::new();
        let mut push = |repo: &RepoRef, detail: &str| {
            if !out.iter().any(|c| c.value.slug == repo.slug) {
                out.push(Choice::new(repo.slug.to_string(), repo.clone()).with_detail(detail));
            }
        };
        if let Some(pane) = &self.pane_repo
            && let Some(Lookup::Ready(Some(repo))) = self.repo_lookups.get(pane)
        {
            push(repo, "this pane's repository");
        }
        if let Some(project) = self.project() {
            for repo in &project.linked_repos {
                push(repo, "linked");
            }
        }
        let mut found: Vec<&RepoRef> = self
            .repo_lookups
            .values()
            .filter_map(|l| match l {
                Lookup::Ready(Some(repo)) => Some(repo),
                _ => None,
            })
            .collect();
        found.sort_by(|a, b| a.slug.cmp(&b.slug));
        for repo in found {
            push(repo, "");
        }
        if let Some(Lookup::Ready(list)) = &self.repos {
            // `list.truncated`: the picker should say the list is partial (type owner/name).
            for repo in &list.repos {
                push(repo, "");
            }
        }
        let loading = matches!(self.repos, Some(Lookup::Loading) | None);
        (out, loading)
    }

    pub(crate) fn open_repo_picker(&mut self, purpose: RepoPurpose) -> Vec<Command> {
        let mut cmds = self.need_repos();
        if let Some(pane) = self.pane_repo.clone() {
            cmds.extend(self.need_repo_lookup(&pane));
        }
        let title = match &purpose {
            RepoPurpose::NewIssue { .. } => "Repository for the new issue",
        };
        self.open_popup(Popup::Repo { purpose, picker: ListPicker::loading(title), wanted: None });
        self.refresh_popup();
        cmds
    }

    fn repo_chosen(&mut self, purpose: RepoPurpose, repo: RepoRef) -> Vec<Command> {
        match purpose {
            RepoPurpose::NewIssue { presets } => {
                vec![Command::Edit { purpose: EditPurpose::NewIssue { repo, presets }, initial: String::new() }]
            }
        }
    }

    pub(crate) fn next_temp(&mut self) -> ItemId {
        self.temp_counter += 1;
        ItemId::new(format!("local:{}", self.temp_counter))
    }
```

New arms in `handle_popup_key`:

```rust
            Popup::NewKind { presets, picker } => match picker.handle(&key) {
                ListOutcome::Chosen(kind) => {
                    let presets = presets.clone();
                    self.close_popup();
                    return match kind {
                        NewKind::Draft => vec![Command::Edit { purpose: EditPurpose::NewDraft { presets }, initial: String::new() }],
                        NewKind::Issue => match check_new(true, &self.edit_context()) {
                            Ok(()) => self.open_repo_picker(RepoPurpose::NewIssue { presets }),
                            Err(denial) => {
                                self.status.flash = Some(self.denial_text(denial));
                                Vec::new()
                            }
                        },
                    };
                }
                ListOutcome::Cancel => {
                    self.close_popup();
                    return Vec::new();
                }
                ListOutcome::None | ListOutcome::Key(_) => {}
            },
            Popup::Repo { purpose, picker, wanted } => {
                // A typed owner/name that is not listed is looked up; once found it is listed
                // first and Enter picks it (the editor only ever starts from a key press).
                if key.code == KeyCode::Enter && picker.current().is_none() {
                    if let Ok(slug) = picker.input.trim().parse::<RepoSlug>() {
                        *wanted = Some(slug.clone());
                        self.popup = Some(popup);
                        let cmds = self.need_repo_lookup(&slug);
                        self.refresh_popup();
                        return cmds;
                    }
                }
                match picker.handle(&key) {
                    ListOutcome::Chosen(repo) => {
                        let purpose = purpose.clone();
                        self.close_popup();
                        return self.repo_chosen(purpose, repo);
                    }
                    ListOutcome::Cancel => {
                        self.close_popup();
                        return Vec::new();
                    }
                    ListOutcome::None | ListOutcome::Key(_) => {}
                }
            }
```

```rust
    /// Where "add existing" searches by default: the pane's repository and the project's
    /// linked ones (spec section 6, "Adding and creating items"). `repo:` in the query widens it.
    pub(crate) fn search_scope(&self) -> Vec<RepoSlug> {
        let mut scope: Vec<RepoSlug> = self.pane_repo.iter().cloned().collect();
        if let Some(p) = self.project() {
            for r in &p.linked_repos {
                if !scope.contains(&r.slug) {
                    scope.push(r.slug.clone());
                }
            }
        }
        scope
    }

    /// Adds the hit, or selects it when it is on the board already.
    fn add_existing(&mut self, hit: ContentHit, presets: Vec<(FieldId, FieldValue)>) -> Vec<Command> {
        let on_board = self.snapshot().and_then(|s| {
            s.items
                .values()
                .find(|i| i.reference().is_some_and(|r| r.repo == hit.repo && r.number == hit.number))
                .map(|i| i.id.clone())
        });
        if let Some(id) = on_board {
            self.status.flash = Some(format!("#{} is already on the board", hit.number));
            self.selected_id = Some(id);
            self.restore_selection();
            return Vec::new();
        }
        let temp = self.next_temp();
        self.submit_new(Op::Create { temp, new: NewItem::Existing { hit }, presets }, false)
    }
```

```rust
            Popup::Search { searched: Some(q), hits, loading, error, selected, .. } => {
                match &self.content_search {
                    Some((query, Lookup::Ready(found))) if query == q => {
                        *hits = found.clone();
                        *loading = false;
                        *selected = 0;

                    Some((query, Lookup::Failed(e))) if query == q => {
```

`src/ui/popup.rs`:

```rust
/// The "add existing" popover: the query, a status line, and the hits as (label, detail).
pub fn render_search(
    frame: &mut Frame,
    area: Rect,
    input: &str,
    status: &str,
    rows: &[(String, String)],
    selected: usize,
    theme: &Theme,
) {
    let rect = centered(area, area.width.saturating_sub(4).min(80), rows.len().clamp(1, 12) as u16 + 4);
    let inner = rect.width.saturating_sub(2) as usize;
    let mut lines = vec![
        Line::styled(format!("> {input}"), theme.accent()),
        Line::styled(truncate_to_width(status, inner), theme.dim()),
    ];
    for (i, (label, detail)) in rows.iter().enumerate().take(12) {
        let style = if i == selected { theme.selected() } else { ratatui::style::Style::default() };
        let detail_w = display_width(detail);
        let label_w = inner.saturating_sub(detail_w + 1);
        lines.push(Line::from(vec![
            Span::styled(pad_to_width(&truncate_to_width(label, label_w), label_w), style),
            Span::styled(" ", style),
            Span::styled(detail.clone(), style.patch(theme.dim())),
        ]));
    }
    boxed(frame, rect, "Add an existing issue or pull request", lines);
}
```

```rust
        Popup::Search { input, searched, hits, selected, loading, error, .. } => {
            let status = match (error, loading, searched) {
                (Some(e), _, _) => e.clone(),
                (None, true, _) => "Searching…".to_string(),
                (None, false, Some(q)) if hits.is_empty() && q == input.trim() => "Nothing found".to_string(),
                _ => "Enter searches (repo:owner/name widens it; a pasted URL works) · Enter again adds".to_string(),
            };
            let rows: Vec<(String, String)> = hits
                .iter()
                .map(|h| {
                    let kind = if h.pull { "PR" } else { "issue" };
                    (format!("{}#{} {}", h.repo, h.number, h.title), kind.to_string())
                })
                .collect();
            crate::ui::popup::render_search(frame, area, input, &status, &rows, *selected, theme)
        }
```

### Task 36: Convert a draft to an issue (`C`)

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/ui/keymap.rs`, `src/ui/app/edit.rs`, `src/ui/app/popups.rs`, the full-screen snapshot

**Interfaces:**

- [ ] **Step 2: Add the issue helper and the tests to `tests/live_edits.rs`**

```rust
/// Runs `body` on a fresh throwaway issue in the testbed repository, added to the testbed
/// project; afterwards removes the item and deletes the issue, whatever happened. Once the
/// issue exists everything, adding it to the project included, runs inside the guard, so a
/// failed add still deletes the issue (pre-flight ruling 22).
async fn with_issue<F, Fut>(name: &str, body: F)
where
    F: FnOnce(Arc<Github>, Project, Item) -> Fut,
    Fut: Future<Output = ()>,
{
    let gh = Arc::new(github(name));
    let project = gh.resolve_project(&testbed().0).await.expect("the testbed resolves");
    let before = gh.rate().remaining;
    let repo = gh.lookup_repo(&testbed_repo()).await.unwrap().expect("the testbed repository is writable");
    let issue = gh
        .create_issue(&repo.id, &format!("pb live: {name}"), "Throwaway issue made by the live suite, which deletes it.")
        .await
        .expect("the throwaway issue is created");
    let added = gh.add_item_by_id(&project.id, &issue).await;
    let outcome = match &added {
        Ok(item) => AssertUnwindSafe(body(gh.clone(), project.clone(), item.clone())).catch_unwind().await,
        Err(e) => Err(Box::new(format!("adding the throwaway issue to the project failed: {e}"))
            as Box<dyn std::any::Any + Send>),
    };
    let cleanup = Github::new(http());
    if let Ok(item) = &added
        && let Err(e) = cleanup.delete_item(&project.id, &item.id).await
    {
        eprintln!("CLEANUP FAILED: remove item {} from the testbed project by hand: {e}", item.id);
    }
    if let Err(e) = cleanup.delete_issue(&issue).await {
        eprintln!("CLEANUP FAILED: delete the issue 'pb live: {name}' from the testbed repository by hand: {e}");
    }
    report_points(name, before, gh.rate().remaining);
    if let Err(panic) = outcome {
        std::panic::resume_unwind(panic);
    }
}

fn content(item: &Item) -> ContentId {
    item.content_id().expect("content id")
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn repository_lookups() {
    let gh = github("edits_lookups"); // not shared: no Arc needed
    let c = gh.repo_candidates(&testbed_repo()).await.unwrap();
    assert!(c.users.iter().any(|u| u.login == "tviles"));
    for name in ["bug", "docs", "good first issue"] {
        assert!(c.labels.iter().any(|l| l.name == name), "label {name}");
    }
    for title in ["v1", "v2"] {
        assert!(c.milestones.iter().any(|m| m.title == title), "milestone {title}");
    }
    assert!(gh.pushable_repos().await.unwrap().iter().any(|r| r.slug == testbed_repo()));
    assert!(gh.lookup_repo(&testbed_repo()).await.unwrap().is_some());
    assert!(gh.lookup_repo(&"tviles/no-such-repo-pb".parse().unwrap()).await.unwrap().is_none());
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn assignees_labels_and_milestone_on_an_issue() {
    with_issue("edits_people", |gh, _p, item| async move {
        let c = gh.repo_candidates(&testbed_repo()).await.unwrap();
        let id = content(&item);
        let me = c.users.iter().find(|u| u.login == "tviles").unwrap();
        let label = |n: &str| c.labels.iter().find(|l| l.name == n).unwrap().id.clone();
        gh.add_assignees(id.node_id(), &[me.id.clone()]).await.unwrap();
        gh.add_labels(id.node_id(), &[label("bug"), label("good first issue")]).await.unwrap();
        gh.remove_labels(id.node_id(), &[label("bug")]).await.unwrap();
        let v1 = c.milestones.iter().find(|m| m.title == "v1").unwrap();
        gh.set_milestone(&id, Some(&v1.id)).await.unwrap();
        let now = reload(&gh, &item.id).await;
        assert_eq!(now.assignees(), ["tviles"]);
        assert_eq!(now.label_names(), ["good first issue"]);
        assert_eq!(now.content_fields.milestone.as_ref().map(|m| m.title.as_str()), Some("v1"));
        gh.remove_assignees(id.node_id(), &[me.id.clone()]).await.unwrap();
        gh.set_milestone(&id, None).await.unwrap();
        let now = reload(&gh, &item.id).await;
        assert!(now.assignees().is_empty());
        assert_eq!(now.content_fields.milestone, None);
    })
    .await;
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn title_body_comment_close_and_reopen_an_issue() {
    with_issue("edits_issue", |gh, _p, item| async move {
        let id = content(&item);
        gh.set_title(&id, "pb live: retitled").await.unwrap();
        gh.set_body(&id, "New body").await.unwrap();
        gh.add_comment(id.node_id(), "A live comment").await.unwrap();
        let detail = gh.fetch_item_detail(&item.id, None).await.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(detail.body, "New body");
        assert!(detail.comments.iter().any(|c| c.body == "A live comment"));
        assert_eq!(reload(&gh, &item.id).await.title(), "pb live: retitled");
        gh.close(&id, Some(CloseReason::NotPlanned)).await.unwrap();
        let closed = reload(&gh, &item.id).await;
        assert!(matches!(closed.content, ItemContent::Issue { state: ContentState::Closed, .. }));
        assert_eq!(closed.content_fields.state_reason, Some(StateReason::NotPlanned));
        gh.reopen(&id).await.unwrap();
        assert!(matches!(reload(&gh, &item.id).await.content, ItemContent::Issue { state: ContentState::Open, .. }));
    })
    .await;
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn archive_and_unarchive() {
    with_issue("edits_archive", |gh, p, item| async move {
        gh.archive_item(&p.id, &item.id).await.unwrap();
        assert!(reload(&gh, &item.id).await.archived);
        gh.unarchive_item(&p.id, &item.id).await.unwrap();
        assert!(!reload(&gh, &item.id).await.archived);
    })
    .await;
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn adding_existing_content_finds_it_by_number_and_returns_the_same_item() {
    with_issue("edits_existing", |gh, p, item| async move {
        let number = item.number().expect("an issue number");
        let hit = gh.content_by_number(&testbed_repo(), number).await.unwrap().expect("found by number");
        assert_eq!(Some(hit.id.clone()), item.content_fields.content_id);
        let again = gh.add_item_by_id(&p.id, &hit.id).await.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(again.id, item.id, "adding content already on the board returns its item");
        // The search index lags writes; only check that searching works.
        gh.search_content(&format!("repo:{} is:open", testbed_repo())).await.unwrap();
    })
    .await;
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn draft_title_body_assignees_and_conversion() {
    with_draft("edits_draft", |gh, p, draft| async move {
        let id = content(&draft);
        let c = gh.repo_candidates(&testbed_repo()).await.unwrap();
        let me = c.users.iter().find(|u| u.login == "tviles").unwrap();
        gh.set_title(&id, "pb live: draft retitled").await.unwrap();
        gh.set_body(&id, "Draft body").await.unwrap();
        gh.set_draft_assignees(id.node_id(), &[me.id.clone()]).await.unwrap();
        let now = reload(&gh, &draft.id).await;
        assert_eq!((now.title(), now.assignees()), ("pb live: draft retitled", vec!["tviles"]));
        let converted = gh.convert_draft(&draft.id, &c.repo.id).await.unwrap();
        let issue = converted.content_fields.content_id.clone().expect("the new issue's id");
        let checks = AssertUnwindSafe(async {
            assert!(matches!(converted.content, ItemContent::Issue { .. }));
            assert_eq!(converted.id, draft.id, "the same project item");
        })
        .catch_unwind()
        .await;
        if let Err(e) = Github::new(http()).delete_issue(&issue).await {
            eprintln!("CLEANUP FAILED: delete the converted issue 'pb live: draft retitled' by hand: {e}");
        }
        if let Err(panic) = checks {
            std::panic::resume_unwind(panic);
        }
        let _ = p;
    })
    .await;
}

/// Uses the seeded "Sample pull request", and puts it back as it was even on failure.
#[tokio::test]
async fn pull_request_title_close_and_reopen() {
    let gh = github("edits_pull");
    let p = gh.resolve_project(&testbed().0).await.unwrap();
    let page = gh.fetch_items_page(&p.id, "", FULL_PAGE, None).await.unwrap();
    let pr = page
        .nodes
        .iter()
        .find(|i| matches!(i.content, ItemContent::PullRequest { .. }))
        .expect("the seeded pull request")
        .clone();
    let id = content(&pr);
    let original = pr.title().to_string();
    let outcome = AssertUnwindSafe(async {
        gh.set_title(&id, &format!("{original} (live)")).await.unwrap();
        gh.close(&id, None).await.unwrap();
        let closed = reload(&gh, &pr.id).await;
        assert!(matches!(closed.content, ItemContent::PullRequest { state: ContentState::Closed, .. }));
        assert_eq!(closed.title(), format!("{original} (live)"));
    })
    .catch_unwind()
    .await;
    let restore = Github::new(http());
    if let Err(e) = restore.reopen(&id).await {
        eprintln!("RESTORE: reopen the sample pull request by hand if it is closed: {e}");
    }
    if let Err(e) = restore.set_title(&id, &original).await {
        eprintln!("RESTORE FAILED: retitle the sample pull request to '{original}' by hand: {e}");
    }
    if let Err(panic) = outcome {
        std::panic::resume_unwind(panic);
    }
}
```

```
Edit the board without leaving herdr: move cards, reorder them, set any field, assign people, label, set milestones, retitle, edit bodies, comment, close and reopen, add new issues, drafts or existing items, convert drafts, archive and remove. Edits show at once and are sent in the background; a failed one is undone and listed in the error log. Saving views and bulk edits arrive in 0.4.
```

```
| `a` / `t` | assignees / labels (type to filter, Space toggles, Enter applies) |
| `c` | comment (opens `$EDITOR`) |
```

```
Titles, bodies, comments and new items open in `$VISUAL` or `$EDITOR` (`vi` if neither is set); the board waits until you close the editor. - [ ] `a` on an issue lists testbed assignees with yours checked; toggling and Enter changes it on github.com.
```
```
- [ ] `t`, type `good`, Space, Enter adds "good first issue"; on a draft `t` says "Drafts have no labels — convert to issue first (`C`)".

- [ ] `A` archives (the item dims, ⊘); `Space` → Show archived items lists it; `A` unarchives.
- [ ] `Space` → Remove from project asks first; the issue stays in the repository.
- [ ] On "Board", `n` in "In Progress" → New draft creates a draft in that column and lane, selected and highlighted.
- [ ] `n` → New issue offers this pane's repository first; the issue appears on github.com in the project.
- [ ] `n` → Add existing finds an issue by text and by pasted URL; one already on the board is selected instead.
- [ ] `C` on a draft converts it to an issue in the chosen repository.
```
